# Overhaul audit findings — 18–19 Oct 2026

Phase 1 of the SpendTrack overhaul. Ten independent read-only auditors (mobile UX, desktop UX, architecture, data integrity & sync, tests, accessibility, security, performance, PWA/platform, product) examined `main` at v0.8.3. A completeness critic then sent six gap probes into areas nobody had covered. Findings were merged by root cause and adversarially verified: P0/P1 by three skeptics with different lenses (reproduce / code truth / impact; a finding survives only if at least two cannot refute it), P2 and gap findings by one skeptic. P3 polish items were not independently verified and are labelled UNVERIFIED.

## Summary

- **The surface is pleasant, but the data layer is not safe.** All six P0s are data-loss or security problems in persistence and sync, and all six were confirmed unanimously.
- **Sync can silently discard edits and never goes idle.** "Clear all" spreads to every device, and anyone holding the public anon key can read and write the relay.
- **Several numbers on screen are wrong:** week and month comparisons, pacing, year averages, and a forecast chart that is always empty. Pulse freezes for 1–8 s at about 641–1,000 entries.
- **The quality gates were hollow:** lint covered 0 app files. Fixed in Track 0 (v0.8.4).
- **Accessibility and phone ergonomics need a focused pass.** Focus is pulled away mid-typing, the date picker cannot be used by keyboard, focus is nearly invisible, and touch targets are small.

| Stage                         | Count   |
| ----------------------------- | ------- |
| Raw findings from 10 auditors | 201     |
| After root-cause merge        | 117     |
| Gap-probe findings added      | 41      |
| **Confirmed**                 | **114** |
| Refuted                       | 1       |
| P3 not independently verified | 43      |
| Unverified (verifier failed)  | 0       |

## Confirmed findings — index

| ID    | Severity    | Area     | Title                                                                                                                                                                                                                | Effort |
| ----- | ----------- | -------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------ |
| F-001 | P0          | Data     | Sync race: any add/edit/delete/undo made while a sync pull is in flight is silently discarded                                                                                                                        | S      |
| F-002 | P0          | Data     | Sync never goes idle: every triggerSync pushes unconditionally and the realtime echo re-triggers it, so devices ping-pong the full payload forever                                                                   | M      |
| F-003 | P0          | Data     | Two open windows/tabs silently erase each other's expenses (whole-array last-writer-wins, no cross-tab awareness)                                                                                                    | M      |
| F-004 | P0          | Data     | Untimestamped tombstones always win: with sync on, 'Clear all' deletes on every device and restoring a JSON backup is silently undone by the next sync                                                               | M      |
| F-005 | P0          | Data     | One malformed record (import, sync or stored) bricks the app at launch: no record validation anywhere and no error boundary                                                                                          | M      |
| F-006 | P0          | Security | Sync relay can be listed and written by anyone holding the public anon key (RLS off + anon grants): full history readable, every linked device can be wiped or bricked                                               | M      |
| G-001 | P0          | Data     | Virtualized Ledger (>150 rows) keys rows by index: a swipe-armed Delete stays on the slot and re-binds to a different expense                                                                                        | S      |
| F-011 | P1          | Data     | Undo of a delete or an edit is silently reverted by the next sync once the change has been pushed                                                                                                                    | M      |
| F-012 | P1          | Data     | Category rename/delete is not sync-safe and leaves learned mappings stale: devices diverge permanently, removed names come back, new entries land in categories that no longer exist                                 | M      |
| F-015 | P1          | Data     | Legacy keyword 'migration' re-runs on every load, sync and import and silently re-files categories the user chose (corrections never stick)                                                                          | S      |
| F-016 | P1          | Data     | Sync failures are invisible or reported as success ('Connected — data synced', 'Synced'); payloads over 1 MB stop syncing forever with no toast                                                                      | S      |
| F-018 | P1          | Data     | Sync ignores app lifecycle: no pull on resume or reconnect, pending push stranded on background, reconnect waits up to 120 s of backoff                                                                              | S      |
| F-019 | P1          | Data     | Quick-add reads Singapore place and brand names as months: entries are saved back-dated, future-dated or without their amount                                                                                        | S      |
| F-020 | P1          | Data     | 'Week' and the insight windows depend on the time of day: before noon they drop today's entries and add an 8th day, and three screens disagree on 'this week'                                                        | M      |
| F-021 | P1          | Data     | 'vs last month' pill compares month-to-date with ALL of last month, showing a misleading green 'down' most of every month (year view likewise)                                                                       | S      |
| F-022 | P1          | Data     | Insight 'Pacing vs last month' drops the last day of last month and can report the opposite direction                                                                                                                | S      |
| F-024 | P1          | Perf     | Pulse recomputes dead O(365 x N) date-parsing analytics on open, tab return and every add/sync: multi-second main-thread freezes at the owner's ~640 entries                                                         | M      |
| F-025 | P1          | Product  | Pace ring is unlabeled and measures all spending against only the budgeted categories ('81%' reads as budget used; a single budget gives a false red 148%)                                                           | S      |
| F-026 | P1          | Product  | Settings copy misstates where data lives when sync is on: 'no cloud, no sharing across devices', a 'locally stored' Clear-all that wipes every linked device, Disconnect, offline banner and iOS-only install text   | S      |
| F-028 | P1          | PWA      | After every deploy, stale lazy chunks crash Ledger/Insights to the 'Unexpected Application Error! Hey developer' page, and the 'App updated, reload' toast dismisses itself instantly                                | S      |
| G-008 | P1          | Data     | Pulse, Ledger and Insights never notice that the day, month or year has changed. They keep showing yesterday's 'Today' numbers, last month's budget and stale insights for days                                      | M      |
| F-007 | P2 (was P1) | A11y     | Sheets move focus to Close on open, overriding the autoFocus field (Ctrl+N then typing logs nothing), and drop focus to <body> on close                                                                              | S      |
| F-008 | P2 (was P1) | A11y     | Date picker cannot be operated by keyboard or screen reader, and Escape while it is open closes the whole Add/Edit sheet and discards the entry                                                                      | M      |
| F-009 | P2 (was P1) | A11y     | Focus indicator is effectively invisible: ~1.2:1 in light, ~1.66:1 in dark, absent in forced-colors mode, and identical to the selected style                                                                        | S      |
| F-013 | P2 (was P1) | Data     | Tombstone cap of 1,000 prunes by insertion order: clear-all of >1,000 entries (or a long-offline device) brings deleted expenses back                                                                                | M      |
| F-014 | P2 (was P1) | Data     | A corrupt or unparseable storage key boots an empty app and deletes the only copy; the next entry then wipes the whole history                                                                                       | S      |
| F-017 | P2 (was P1) | Data     | The last expense is not saved when the app is backgrounded: the 400 ms save debounce is flushed only on pagehide/beforeunload, never on visibilitychange                                                             | S      |
| F-023 | P2 (was P1) | Data     | Year view shows two contradictory 'monthly average' figures on the same screen                                                                                                                                       | S      |
| F-027 | P2 (was P1) | Product  | Demo build re-seeds 59 fake expenses (and demo budgets) whenever the store is empty: after Clear all + reload, and with no sync guard                                                                                | S      |
| F-029 | P2 (was P1) | PWA      | Installed iPhone PWA: the top bar draws under the status bar / Dynamic Island (safe-area-inset-top never applied)                                                                                                    | S      |
| F-030 | P2 (was P1) | UX       | Any parent re-render while a Sheet is open re-runs its focus effect and yanks focus to Close: keystrokes are lost mid-typing and a typed space closes the add sheet                                                  | S      |
| F-031 | P2 (was P1) | UX       | Insights '14-day forecast' chart always renders empty (bars are 0px tall) at every viewport                                                                                                                          | S      |

## Confirmed findings — detail (P0–P2)

### F-001 · P0 · Data — Sync race: any add/edit/delete/undo made while a sync pull is in flight is silently discarded

- **Where:** src/stores/useSyncStore.ts:124, :129-143 (exportBackup snapshot at :130 before await pullSync at :131, applySync at :138, push at :143); src/stores/useExpenseStore.ts:388-402 (applySync replaces the whole store); src/stores/useSyncStore.ts:43 (3 s push debounce); src/app/AppShell.tsx:63 (initSync pulls on every open)
- **Impact:** Permanent, silent data loss in the main daily flow with sync ON (production): entries vanish after the success toast, deletes come back, edits revert, on every synced device, and nothing reports an error. initSync pulls on every app open, every realtime event triggers another pull, and the 3 s push debounce fires mid-way through multi-entry logging, so open-and-log and receipt-style logging land in the window. With two devices open a pull is in flight most of the time (see the sync echo loop), making loss routine. Unrecoverable unless a manual JSON export predates it.
- **Fix:** Never apply a snapshot taken before an await. After `await pullSync`, re-read `useExpenseStore.getState().exportBackup()`, merge and applySync in the same synchronous tick (no await between merge and set), then push that result. Add a dirty flag: if scheduleSyncPush fires while in flight, re-run triggerSync after the in-flight one resolves instead of returning the stale promise (useSyncStore.ts:124); if state changed during the push, schedule another round. Add a regression test that mutates during a delayed fake pull (the s1 / fake-supabase / race.probe harnesses are ready templates).
- **Evidence:** [ARC-01] Ran the REAL useSyncStore.triggerSync against an in-memory Supabase fake with 300 ms latency (scratch/harness/sync-tests.ts, fake-supabase.ts). During the pull, addExpense("Grab to camp") and deleteExpense("e1") both applied locally. After the sync resolved: local = ["Chicken rice"], remote = ["Chicken rice"], deletedIds = []. The add is gone and the delete is undone (scratch/out/sync-tests.log, F_realTriggerSyncRace). [DAT-01] Real store/sync code bundled by esbuild against a fake Supabase client (<scratch>/harness/s1-add-during-pull.cjs, s1.log): 'Grab home' added 15 ms into an 800 ms pull -> store=Lunch, localStorage=Lunch, cloud=Lunch => 'RESULT: DATA LOSS'. s6.log: a delete during the pull is resurrected ('Taxi present? true') and an edit 7->70 is reverted to 7 in memory and cloud. s3.log (20 entries at human pace 2-8 s apart, 250 ms RTT): 1 device lost 3/1/1 of 20 across 3 seeds; phone+laptop both open lost 5/8/6 of 20. [TST-01] Probe TQ/probe/race.probe.test.ts runs the real useExpenseStore + useSyncStore + mergeBackups, mocking only the network: addExpense('chicken rice') during the pull, then the pull resolves -> store ids ['r1'], pushed ids ['r1']; the added id is gone from both after the 'Added' toast (TQ/race-probe.log). The persistence subscriber then saves the reduced state.
- **Verification:** code: confirmed (P0); reproduce: confirmed (P0); impact: confirmed (P0). Sources: ARC-01, DAT-01, TST-01.

### F-002 · P0 · Data — Sync never goes idle: every triggerSync pushes unconditionally and the realtime echo re-triggers it, so devices ping-pong the full payload forever

- **Where:** src/stores/useSyncStore.ts:95-101 (every realtime event -> triggerSync), :119-157, :143 (always pushes, even when nothing changed); src/services/syncService.ts:165 (fresh exportDate on every merge), :180-199 (upsert), :229-234 (no own-write filter); src/stores/useExpenseStore.ts:388-402 (applySync always replaces expenses via migrateExpenses().map), :447-460 (400 ms persist debounce re-armed by every applySync); supabase/migrations/001_sync.sql:14,26 (REPLICA IDENTITY FULL + table in supabase_realtime publication)
- **Impact:** With phone + laptop (or two tabs) open, a pull is almost always in flight, which turns the pull race into routine loss (s3.log: 25-40% of entries lost with two devices open). Both devices re-download and re-upload the whole snapshot (~127 KB at ~640 entries) in a loop; each cycle replaces `expenses`, so Pulse redoes its multi-second analytics and Ledger/Insights re-render, pegging the phone and draining battery and mobile data. Local persistence can be starved until pagehide. It also burns Supabase realtime/egress quota.
- **Fix:** Make sync idempotent. (1) Skip the push when the merged content equals the remote content (hash of expenses/categories/mappings/budgets/tombstones excluding exportDate, or a monotonic revision). (3) Use the event's `payload.new` instead of a blind re-pull. (4) Do not change exportDate on no-op merges. (5) In applySync keep the existing array/objects when nothing changed (structural sharing by id + updatedAt) so subscribers do not fire, and flush persistence directly after applySync instead of re-arming the debounce. Regression test with the sync-sim mock: after one add, two devices must reach a quiescent state with at most 2 upserts.
- **Evidence:** [DAT-02] Every upsert raises a postgres_changes UPDATE to every subscriber; the callback runs triggerSync unconditionally; triggerSync always upserts a new row (exportDate/updated_at always change), which raises the next event. Real code over 10 s of ZERO user activity (<scratch>/harness/s2-echo-loop.cjs, s2.log): 1 device, event after HTTP response -> 17 pushes + 17 pulls; 2 devices -> 34 pushes (event after response) and 26 pushes (event before response; the cross-device ping-pong does not depend on self-echo timing). Device A rewrote all localStorage keys 17-34 times in those 10 s. ~238 B per expense, so ~240 KB per transfer at 1,000 expenses. [PRF-02] REAL store and sync code against a mock Supabase with realtime fan-out (sync-sim/\*.ts, bundled with esbuild). Single device with self-echo: one SmartInput-style add gives 69 upserts + 70 pulls in 15 s (8.76 MB up / 8.75 MB down at ~640 entries), still 'syncing' at the end, 70 new `expenses` array identities, and localStorage.setItem for data called 0 times in 15 s because the persist debounce is starved (sync-sim/sim-~640.out.json). Two devices with self-echo disabled: 28 upserts in the first 3 s after both connect with no user action, 102 upserts in 13 s; each device saw 51-52 expenses identity changes (sync-sim/two-devices-noselfecho.out.json). Not observed against production Supabase (no network allowed); the loop logic is deterministic.
- **Verification:** code: confirmed (P0); reproduce: confirmed (P0); impact: confirmed (P0). Sources: DAT-02, PRF-02.

### F-003 · P0 · Data — Two open windows/tabs silently erase each other's expenses (whole-array last-writer-wins, no cross-tab awareness)

- **Where:** src/stores/useExpenseStore.ts:429-441 (persistCurrentState writes the whole in-memory slice); src/services/storage.ts:83 (saveExpenses = JSON.stringify(all)), :188-204 (saveAll rewrites whole arrays); no 'storage' event / BroadcastChannel / Web Locks anywhere in src
- **Impact:** Silent loss of logged expenses whenever the app is open twice on one device: an installed desktop PWA window plus a browser tab, two laptop tabs, or a Chrome tab plus the installed app on Android (they share storage). Deletions in one window come back too, and the stale window shows wrong totals. With sync ON, the cloud merge repairs most of it, but not if window A is closed within the 3 s push debounce (useSyncStore.ts:43); two tabs also become two sync clients, which feeds the sync echo loop. Users without sync lose data permanently.
- **Fix:** Merge on write. Before persisting, read the stored snapshot and merge it with the in-memory state using the existing pure mergeBackups() (syncService.ts:50; move it to src/lib). Listen for window 'storage' events on the persisted keys and rehydrate/merge into the store, with a guard like syncApplying so the write does not echo back. Better still, elect a single writer with navigator.locks or BroadcastChannel and make writes read-modify-write per record. Add manifest launch_handler {client_mode: 'focus-existing'} so desktop/Android launches reuse the open window. Add an e2e test from multitab.cjs.
- **Evidence:** [PWA-01] pwa-platform/multitab.cjs -> multitab.log on the :4173 build: tab A quick-adds 'tab A coffee' and localStorage = ['tab A coffee']; tab B (opened before that) does not show it (tabBShowsTabAExpense=false), then quick-adds 'tab B lunch' and localStorage = ['tab B lunch']; reopening the app shows only ['tab B lunch'], so tabAExpenseLost=true. grep for 'storage' event, BroadcastChannel and navigator.locks in src: none. [DAT-08] Real browser, prod build :4173 (<scratch>/b1-two-tabs.cjs, b1.log): tab1 adds 'coffee', tab2 adds 'lunch' (localStorage=[lunch]), tab1 adds 'bus' -> localStorage=[bus, coffee]; after reload 'lunch' is gone. Screenshot <scratch>/b1-two-tabs-after-reload.png.
- **Verification:** code: confirmed (P0); reproduce: confirmed (P0); impact: confirmed (P0). Sources: PWA-01, DAT-08.

### F-004 · P0 · Data — Untimestamped tombstones always win: with sync on, 'Clear all' deletes on every device and restoring a JSON backup is silently undone by the next sync

- **Where:** src/stores/useExpenseStore.ts:267-274 (clearAllExpenses tombstones every id), :357-371 (importBackup replaces deletedIds with the file's); src/services/syncService.ts:54-62 (tombstones unioned, always win), :155 (cap slices by Set insertion order); src/app/AppShell.tsx:40-58 (push on deletedIds change); src/features/settings/SettingsDrawer.tsx:466-470 ('This will replace all current data'), :514 (confirm text)
- **Impact:** The documented recovery path (export, clear, import) silently destroys the restored data on every synced device about 3 s later, after a success toast ('Imported N expenses'), for anyone with sync on (the owner's production setup). Clearing data from a borrowed or shared laptop wipes Shade's phone, and Clear is not a privacy wipe of the device either.
- **Fix:** Store tombstones as {id: deletedAt} (migrate the array using the epoch as the date). In mergeBackups an expense whose updatedAt is newer than its tombstone survives; prune tombstones by age. importBackup stamps updatedAt=now on restored records and removes their ids from tombstones (local and on the next push), or offers an explicit 'Restore everywhere' with a forced rev and a replace-vs-merge preview.
- **Evidence:** [ARC-02] Real store + real mergeBackups: 3 expenses -> Clear all -> sync -> import the JSON exported before clearing -> after import all 3 are present -> next sync -> '(empty)'. Remote tombstones are [e1,e2,e3] (scratch/out/sync-tests.log, C_clearThenImport). The 1000-tombstone cap prunes by insertion order, not age, contrary to its comment. [DAT-06] s4.log C5: clear-all (propagated to cloud) -> import the backup file -> 'local right after import: 2 expenses' -> 'after the next sync (3 s later): 0 expenses <- restore silently undone'. 'Replace' is also not a replace: entries that exist only on the remote come back through the union. [SEC-03] Real merge simulation (security/t11_clear_restore.cjs -> t11_output.log): with 300 expenses, after the clear the remote bucket has 0, the phone after its next pull has 0, and after a JSON restore plus sync the device has 0. With 1500 expenses the cap leaves 500 on the phone and restores 500 (half-deleted). The confirmation says 'This permanently removes all locally stored expense data' and never mentions other devices. security/t7_clear_residue.cjs (t7_output.log): after a clear the store still keeps learned item names in categoryMappings, budgets, the syncId and all tombstones, so reconnecting that device later would wipe the bucket again. Not run live across two devices (local builds have sync disabled).
- **Verification:** code: confirmed (P0); reproduce: confirmed (P0); impact: confirmed (P0). Sources: ARC-02, DAT-06, SEC-03.

### F-005 · P0 · Data — One malformed record (import, sync or stored) bricks the app at launch: no record validation anywhere and no error boundary

- **Where:** src/app/routes.tsx:31-59 (createBrowserRouter, no errorElement; no ErrorBoundary in src); src/services/storage.ts:61-80 (:66, :73 Array.isArray then `as Expense[]`); src/services/syncService.ts:50, :195-211 (:210 payload cast to BackupData, never validated); src/features/settings/SettingsDrawer.tsx:388-409 (:395 date only checked with typeof string); src/lib/analytics.ts:167-168 (NaN passes range guard, format() throws); src/stores/useExpenseStore.ts:357-371, :388-402; src/stores/useSyncStore.ts:147-151
- **Impact:** A bad backup file, a future client bug, or anyone with relay access can lock the owner out of his data on every device at once. Settings (and therefore export) is unreachable, the default React Router page offers no recovery, and in the installed PWA the obvious fix (clear site data) deletes everything. One poisoned row spreads to all devices through sync.
- **Fix:** Write one hand-rolled validator (sanitizeBackup / sanitizeExpense in src/domain or src/services/validate.ts; no zod, given ~47 KB of bundle headroom) and use it at import, hydrate and pullSync/applySync: id string 1-64 and unique; itemName string 1-200; amount finite with 0 < x <= 1e7; date/createdAt/updatedAt must pass Date.parse/isValid(parseISO); notes string up to 500 or absent; category string 1-60 and not a reserved prototype name; categories string[]; mappings/budgets rebuilt as validated null-prototype records with numeric budgets; deletedIds string[]; reject unknown major versions. For sync and hydrate, drop/repair and count bad records, quarantine them under a separate key and toast, rather than rejecting the whole payload or crashing. Unit-test the validator with the t2/b3 fixtures and add an e2e test that seeds a poisoned row and asserts the shell survives.
- **Evidence:** [ARC-03] Chromium on :4173 with one stored expense missing itemName: the whole UI is replaced by 'Unexpected Application Error! Cannot read properties of undefined (reading 'charAt')', a stack trace and the '💿 Hey developer 👋' hint (scratch/shots/malformed-record.png; scratch/out/browser-checks.log malformedRecord). mergeBackups throws `TypeError: Cannot read properties of undefined (reading 'map')` on a remote payload without expenses[] (out/sync-tests.log H_malformedRemote), so sync loops in error/retry. [DAT-10] Imported through the real UI (<scratch>/b3-import.cjs, b3.log, b3b.log): date 'last tuesday' -> Pulse and Insights crash with 'Invalid time value' on every load, Settings unreachable. A category list containing an object, or notes as an object -> Ledger crash (React #31). Budget as the string '300' -> 'Food & Dining $4.50 / $0.00' (b3-budget-as-string-pulse.png). Amount 1e9 accepted. A stored record missing `date` -> 'Unexpected Application Error!' at launch (b2.log). [TST-02] Real UI flow on :4173 (TQ/import-baddate.cjs): one row date '18/10/2026' is accepted ('Import 2 expenses?'); /pulse and /insights render 'Unexpected Application Error! Invalid time value RangeError' with no TopBar, TabBar or Settings (TQ/import-baddate-pulse.png, TQ/import-baddate.log); survives reload; PWA start_url is /pulse so every launch crashes. bugs-fixed.md:32 claims deep date validation that the code does not do. [SEC-02] security/t2_import_poison.cjs (t2_output.log, screenshot t2_date_garbage.png): date 'not-a-date' accepted and crashes Pulse/Insights across reloads; notes {x:1} or categories [{evil:1}] crash the Ledger (React #31). t3_output.log: a remote payload with an invalid date, a notes object, amount 'abc', amount -1e9 and a category object passes through mergeBackups
- **Verification:** reproduce: confirmed (P0); impact: confirmed (P0); code: confirmed (P0). Sources: ARC-03, DAT-10, TST-02, SEC-02.

### F-006 · P0 · Security — Sync relay can be listed and written by anyone holding the public anon key (RLS off + anon grants): full history readable, every linked device can be wiped or bricked

- **Where:** supabase/migrations/001_sync.sql:14,26 (REPLICA IDENTITY FULL + supabase_realtime publication), :32 (DISABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY), :35-36 (GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE TO anon/authenticated); src/services/syncService.ts:12-13, :34-38, :55-62, :195-211 (pulled payload trusted with no validation), :227-231; dist/assets/index-\*.js (sync code ships in the main chunk); README.md:24 + conductor/product.md:14 (prod URLs are public)
- **Impact:** Anyone can open the public deployment, copy the anon key from its JS, and read Shade's whole spending history (item names, notes, amounts, dates) along with the sync codes, then silently delete, rewrite or brick that data on every synced device. The 'leave RLS off' decision assumed you need the code to read; you don't. This is the largest possible blast radius for both privacy and data integrity.
- **Fix:** Keep RLS off and keep the code as the shared secret, but stop exposing the table directly. Migration 004 (run supabase-migration-safety first): BEGIN; REVOKE ALL ON public.sync_buckets FROM anon, authenticated; ALTER PUBLICATION supabase_realtime DROP TABLE public.sync_buckets; ALTER TABLE public.sync_buckets REPLICA IDENTITY DEFAULT; CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.sync_pull(p_sync_id uuid) RETURNS jsonb LANGUAGE sql STABLE SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = '' AS $$ SELECT payload FROM public.sync_buckets WHERE sync_id = p_sync_id::text $$; CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.sync_push(p_sync_id uuid, p_payload jsonb) RETURNS void LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = '' AS $$ BEGIN IF pg_column_size(p_payload) > 1048576 OR jsonb_typeof(p_payload->'expenses') IS DISTINCT FROM 'array' THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'invalid payload' USING ERRCODE = '22023'; END IF; INSERT INTO public.sync_buckets (sync_id, payload, updated_at) VALUES (p_sync_id::text, p_payload, now()) ON CONFLICT (sync_id) DO UPDATE SET payload = EXCLUDED.payload, updated_at = now(); END $$; REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.sync_pull(uuid), public.sync_push(uuid, jsonb), public.prune_stale_sync_buckets(interval) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated; GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.sync_pull(uuid), public.sync_push(uuid, jsonb) TO anon, authenticated; COMMIT;. Optionally add a base_rev compare-and-swap to sync_push. Client side: pullSync becomes client.rpc('sync_pull', { p_sync_id }) and pushSync becomes client.rpc('sync_push', { p_sync_id, p_payload }). Replace the postgres_changes subscription with a Broadcast channel 'sync:'+uuid (send {event:'changed'} after each successful push; receivers call triggerSync). Validate every pulled payload with the same validator as import before merging. Deploy order: (1) client using the RPCs, (2) the migration, (3) rotate the sync code and treat the current one as leaked. Second layer: end-to-end AES-GCM encryption so the relay only holds ciphertext.
- **Evidence:** [SEC-01] 001_sync.sql:32 runs DISABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY and :35 runs GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE ... TO anon. With RLS off and SELECT granted, PostgREST returns every row for GET /rest/v1/sync_buckets?select=sync_id,payload, with no sync_id filter needed: the sync code is a column the reader gets back, not a secret. syncService.ts:12-13 inlines VITE_SUPABASE_URL and VITE_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY, and the 'sync_buckets' literal is in dist/assets/index-yU4QgA3X.js, so every visitor downloads the key. REPLICA IDENTITY FULL plus the publication let an unfiltered subscriber receive every bucket's full row on each push. The UPDATE grant lets the same reader overwrite any bucket. Real mergeBackups (security/t3_merge_poison.cjs -> t3_output.log): a remote payload whose deletedIds lists all 500 local ids merges to 0 expenses and {} budgets, and the tombstone union spreads the wipe; a remote copy with updatedAt '9999-12-31' always wins LWW (amount rewritten to 99999). backlog.md:31 records RLS as left as-is on 2026-06-13. To confirm: curl "$VITE_SUPABASE_URL/rest/v1/sync_buckets?select=sync_id,updated_at&limit=3" -H "apikey: $VITE_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY"; any rows returned means confirmed. [DAT-03] Same grants; integrity consequences reproduced locally: a payload that tombstones every id empties every device on its next merge (mergeBackups unions tombstones, syncService.ts:55-62; C5 in s4.log), and a single record without a parseable date crashes every device at launch (b2.log 'element-missing-date' -> 'Unexpected Application Error!'; b3.log 'date-not-a-date' -> Pulse CRASH). Neither auditor could test the live Supabase project (no network).
- **Verification:** code: confirmed (P0); impact: confirmed (P0); reproduce: confirmed (P0). Sources: SEC-01, DAT-03.

### G-001 · P0 · Data — Virtualized Ledger (>150 rows) keys rows by index: a swipe-armed Delete stays on the slot and re-binds to a different expense

- **Where:** src/features/ledger/VirtualLedgerList.tsx:35-40 (no getItemKey), :52 (key={vi.key}); src/features/ledger/LedgerRow.tsx:19,25,26 (x / revealed / dragStarted are per-instance state), :42-50, :65
- **Impact:** In the owner's Year and All views (~640 entries) and any view over 150 rows, a reflex double tap (about 78%) or any later tap on a Delete that is still open removes an expense the user never chose. The only recovery is the 4 s toast Undo. The deletion is then tombstoned to every synced device and totals silently drop.
- **Fix:** Pass getItemKey: (i) => flat[i].kind === 'header' ? 'h:' + flat[i].date : 'r:' + flat[i].expense.id to useWindowVirtualizer and keep key={vi.key}. Lift swipe state into the list as a single openRowId. Clear it whenever `flat` changes, on scroll start and after any delete, so at most one row is armed and no armed state can outlive its record. Port the a1/b harness into the Playwright e2e suite as a regression test.
- **Evidence:** virtual-core 3.17 defaults to getItemKey = index (node*modules/@tanstack/virtual-core/dist/esm/index.js:18,258). VirtualLedgerList passes no getItemKey, so each slot div is keyed by its index. LedgerRow keeps the swipe offset, revealed flag and dragStarted in the component instance, and React reuses that instance for whichever expense lands on the index. Reproduced with real touch: Chromium uses CDP Input.dispatchTouchEvent drags. WebKit runs the iPhone 13 profile, with drags sent as PointerEvent pointerType 'touch' and taps through page.touchscreen.tap. (1) Double tap: swipe a row, tap its Delete, then tap the same point again 150-1000 ms later. In 39/50 trials the second tap deleted a record that was never swiped (Chromium 20/25, WebKit 19/25; an earlier WebKit run gave 21/25). Datasets: 300 rows in the current month, and ~640 entries in the Year and All views (a1_double_tap_trials*_.json, a1\__.log). In 37 of those 39 the finger was on a real, visible 'Delete <next item>' button (translateX -96 px, opacity 0.904, shots/a1*webkit_month300_virtual_before2nd.png). The 11 non-carry trials were rows that were last in their day group, where the slot became a header. All 10 tap positions across the 96 px Delete footprint deleted a second record on both engines (a3_footprint.json). (2) Remaps without a double tap, 1/1 on each engine (b_shift_scenarios*\*.json). Quick-add 'coffee 4.5 today' while Item 037 is armed: the armed slot now shows Item 018 (the row above) and Item 037 slides down closed. Tapping the visible Delete deletes Item 018 (shots/b1_webkit_after_quickadd.png). Undo after a swipe-delete leaves a never-swiped row armed (Item 056 / Item 018), and the next tap deletes it. Category filter All to Food & Dining (335 rows): Exp 449 was swiped, then Exp 047 was armed an
- **Verification:** code: confirmed (P0); impact: confirmed (P0); reproduce: confirmed (P0). Sources: GAP1-01.

### F-011 · P1 · Data — Undo of a delete or an edit is silently reverted by the next sync once the change has been pushed

- **Where:** src/stores/useExpenseStore.ts:208, :225-265 (undo closures restore `prev` with its old updatedAt; undo-delete removes the tombstone locally only), :232-241, :238-239, :245-265, :256-261 (5 s undo window); src/stores/useSyncStore.ts:43 (3 s push debounce); src/ui/ToastHost.tsx:19 (4 s Undo toast); src/services/syncService.ts:55-75
- **Impact:** On synced devices the toast's Undo visibly works, then quietly reverses itself about 3 s later: deleted entries stay deleted and wrong amounts come back. This breaks the qa-checklist rule 'Undo works for every new destructive action'.
- **Fix:** Implement undo as a forward write. Edit-undo writes the previous fields with updatedAt = now (`{...prev, updatedAt: now}`). Make deletion LWW per id with timestamped tombstones {id, deletedAt}; an expense survives when updatedAt > deletedAt, so undo-delete re-creates the record with updatedAt = now. Alternatively keep local tombstones out of pushes until the undo window closes. Add regression tests.
- **Evidence:** [ARC-06] Real store + mergeBackups (scratch/out/sync-tests.log): A) delete -> sync -> Undo -> local shows 'Chicken rice:4.5' -> next sync -> '(empty)'. B) edit 4.5 -> 45 -> sync -> Undo -> local 4.5 -> next sync -> 45 again (prev carries the older updatedAt, so LWW picks the remote edit). bugs-fixed.md describes the budget fix ('carry updatedAt/deletedAt and prefer the newer side'); it was never applied to expenses. [DAT-05] s4.log C3: delete e1, push, Undo -> 'local after undo has e1: true' then 'after next sync has e1: false (tombstone wins)'. C7: edit 1.5->15, push, Undo -> local 1.5, after sync 15. The toast lives 4 s but the push fires at 3 s (or immediately under the echo loop). [TST-04] TQ/probe/sync.probe.test.ts: deleteExpense -> push snapshot -> consumeUndo: local 1 row, after the next merge 0 rows (tombstone union wins). updateExpense(amount 99) -> push -> undo restores 10, after the next merge 99 (TQ/probe-run.log).
- **Verification:** reproduce: confirmed (P1); code: confirmed (P1); impact: confirmed (P1). Sources: ARC-06, DAT-05, TST-04.

### F-012 · P1 · Data — Category rename/delete is not sync-safe and leaves learned mappings stale: devices diverge permanently, removed names come back, new entries land in categories that no longer exist

- **Where:** src/stores/useExpenseStore.ts:159-175 and :186-187 (getSmartCategory), :282-320 (renameCategory: no updatedAt bump, no mapping update, no budgetDeletedAt for the old key), :294, :322-334 (removeCategory: no updatedAt, mappings untouched), :326; src/services/syncService.ts:73-75 (LWW tie -> local wins), :82 (categories = plain union, no tombstones), :86 (mappings local-wins)
- **Impact:** Phone and laptop show different category totals and budget pace for the same data, and the Pulse budget ring can use a doubled budget. Renamed or deleted categories keep coming back. New expenses land in hidden, orphaned categories that cannot be filtered or budgeted.
- **Fix:** Rename/remove/merge must stamp updatedAt=now on every touched expense, rewrite categoryMappings values in the same action (from -> target on rename; drop or point to 'Other' on remove), and write budgetDeletedAt for the old key. Model categories as keyed records with updatedAt/deletedAt (or categoryDeletedAt/categoryRenames maps, payload v4.1) merged LWW with tombstones like budgets. Add merge tests for rename and remove in both orderings, and unit tests for the mapping remap.
- **Evidence:** [ARC-07] Real store + mergeBackups (scratch/out/sync-tests.log D/E, out/domain-tests.log): rename 'Food & Dining' -> 'Meals' on A and sync: A's list becomes ['Meals','Transportation','Other','Food & Dining'] (old name back). B keeps 'Food & Dining' on both expenses: equal timestamps, so each side's local copy wins forever. Deleted 'Transportation' reappears after sync. Mappings still say {'chicken rice': 'Food & Dining'}, so a full-form add files into a category no longer in the list. [DAT-07] s4.log C1: after renaming 'Food & Dining'->'Food' on A and two sync rounds, A shows e1 as 'Food' and B 'Food & Dining' (permanent split); the list holds both and budgets become {Food:300,'Food & Dining':300}. C6: total monthly budget 300 -> 600. C2: a deleted category comes back and B keeps the expense in it. s6.log: after rename a new 'kopi' gets 'Food & Dining' (mapping not updated); same after deleting 'Transportation'. [TST-03] TQ/probe/sync.probe.test.ts: after renameCategory('Food','Meals') the renamed row has updatedAt=undefined; stale B keeps ['Food','Food'], A keeps ['Meals','Meals'], categories ['Meals','Other','Food']. removeCategory('Gym') + merge -> ['Food','Other','Gym'] (TQ/probe-run.log). [TST-16] Probe: rename 'Food' to 'Meals', then addExpense('coffee') -> category 'Food' while categories = ['Meals','Other']. Remove 'Gym', then addExpense('gym pass') -> 'Gym' (TQ/probe-run.log).
- **Verification:** reproduce: confirmed (P1); code: confirmed (P1); impact: confirmed (P1). Sources: ARC-07, DAT-07, TST-03, TST-16.

### F-015 · P1 · Data — Legacy keyword 'migration' re-runs on every load, sync and import and silently re-files categories the user chose (corrections never stick)

- **Where:** src/utils/helpers.ts:48-68 (migrateExpenseCategory, substring match); src/constants.ts:18-21 (flat renames, e.g. Education -> Subscriptions, Bills & Utilities -> Travel), :24-152 (ITEM_CATEGORY_PATTERNS keyword overrides); src/stores/useExpenseStore.ts:110 (hydrate), :359 (importBackup), :390 (every applySync)
- **Impact:** Category totals, Top categories and budget pace are wrong in ways the user cannot fix, and nothing tells them it happened. User-created categories like 'Education' or 'Bills & Utilities' can never be used, corrections are undone on every open and sync, and sync spreads the rewrite to other devices.
- **Fix:** Make it a real one-time migration: store a schemaVersion key, migrate only legacy data once, persist and bump updatedAt; never call it inside applySync or importBackup for current-version data (or remove it, since its target categories were renamed long ago). If keyword rules are kept, match whole words and only on entries never edited by the user. Test that migrate(migrate(x)) == migrate(x) and that user categories survive hydrate.
- **Evidence:** [ARC-08] Real store hydrate (scratch/out/domain-tests.log): all 7 deliberately filed test items changed category: 'Kitchen towel' Other->Shopping ('kit'), 'GitHub shares' Investment->Subscriptions, 'Ear wax removal clinic' Healthcare->Grooming, 'Board game cafe' and 'Cod fish & chips' Social & Leisure->Gaming, 'Electric shaver' Healthcare->Grooming, 'Pool table fee' Other->Social & Leisure. After editing 'Kitchen towel' back to Other, the next applySync makes it Shopping again; no updatedAt is stamped. [DAT-11] Pure run (<scratch>/harness/s5.log section 6): 'Kit Kat' Other->Shopping, 'Kitchen towels'->Shopping, 'Steamboat dinner' Social & Leisure->Gaming ('steam'), 'Public toilet' Other->Social & Leisure ('pub'), 'Movie with friends lol'->Gaming ('lol'), 'Trimmer blades'->Grooming ('trim'). Real browser (b4.log 1): after reload and one add the stored categories were rewritten. Unmapped new items default to 'Other' (useExpenseStore.ts:187), so ordinary entries are affected. [TST-05] TQ/probe/logic.probe.test.ts with the real migrateExpenseCategory: 'SQL course' in user category 'Education' -> Subscriptions; 'football game tickets' -> Gaming; 'ear wax removal' -> Grooming; 'kitkat' -> Shopping; 'club sandwich' -> Social & Leisure; 'ikea kitchen stool' -> Shopping (TQ/probe-run.log). No version flag gates it. [PRD-01] Browser repro (walk3.cjs / walk3.log.json / migration-pulse.png, :4173): Pulse showed 'SP Group electricity' under Travel (stored as Bills & Utilities), 'Board game night' under Gaming, 'Carpool to camp' under Social & Leisure (stored as Other). After the user edited 'Board game night' back and reloaded, storage said Social & Leisure but the UI showed Gaming again. hydrate() runs before installPersistence() subscribes, so migrated categories live in memory un
- **Verification:** code: confirmed (P1); reproduce: confirmed (P1); impact: confirmed (P1). Sources: ARC-08, DAT-11, TST-05, PRD-01.

### F-016 · P1 · Data — Sync failures are invisible or reported as success ('Connected — data synced', 'Synced'); payloads over 1 MB stop syncing forever with no toast

- **Where:** src/stores/useSyncStore.ts:119-157, :147-151 (catch sets cloudStatus=error, never rethrows, so triggerSync never rejects); src/features/settings/SyncPanel.tsx:111-129 (handleConnect), :142-149 (handleManualSync); src/services/syncService.ts:186-193 (PayloadTooLargeError thrown); src/app/SyncPill.tsx:17-19 (top-bar pill shows only local save state)
- **Impact:** The owner can be told his data synced when it did not, which is dangerous right before switching or wiping a device. Devices quietly diverge for weeks while every indicator says Saved/Synced.
- **Fix:** triggerSync returns {ok, error} (or rethrows after setting status) and the handlers branch on it. Show cloud status in the top-bar pill (synced / offline / failing since X). Toast PayloadTooLargeError and network errors with their text, e.g. 'Export and archive old years'. Compress or split the payload, or move to per-record rows, before the limit.
- **Evidence:** [ARC-11] With the fake transport failing (scratch/out/sync-tests.log G_errorSwallowed): setupSync resolved (threw=false), triggerSync resolved (threw=false), and cloudStatus = 'error'. Both SyncPanel handlers show their success toast whenever the await does not throw. [DAT-17] Measured realistic record = 238 bytes (m1.log; demo average 174 B): warn at about 3,442 expenses, hard fail at about 4,406 (about 2.4 years at 5 per day). bugs-fixed.md claims 'reject writes > 1 MB with a user-facing toast'; no such toast exists (grep: PayloadTooLargeError is only thrown). handleManualSync awaits triggerSync, which resolves even on error, so 'Synced' is always shown.
- **Verification:** code: confirmed (P1); reproduce: confirmed (P1); impact: confirmed (P1). Sources: ARC-11, DAT-17.

### F-018 · P1 · Data — Sync ignores app lifecycle: no pull on resume or reconnect, pending push stranded on background, reconnect waits up to 120 s of backoff

- **Where:** src/stores/useSyncStore.ts:43 (3 s push debounce), :159-168, :175-189 (backoff to 120 s); src/services/syncService.ts:231-246 (realtime re-subscribe does not triggerSync); src/stores/useExpenseStore.ts:487 ('online' only flips a flag). Sync triggers found by grep: startup, realtime event, local change, retry, manual button. None for visibilitychange, focus or online.
- **Impact:** Phone plus laptop: expenses logged on the laptop do not appear when the phone app is resumed from the background. Totals stay stale until a local edit or a cold start. An expense logged on the phone and pocketed within 3 s stays off the laptop until the phone app is next resumed. After coming back online, the copy promises sync, but it can lag about 2 minutes.
- **Fix:** On visibilitychange to visible and on window 'online': clear the retry timer, reset retryAttempt and call triggerSync() (throttled to about once per 30 s). On visibilitychange to hidden: flush the pending push immediately (triggerSync without waiting for the 3 s debounce). On realtime status SUBSCRIBED after a reconnect: triggerSync() to catch missed events.
- **Evidence:** Read from code; could not run, because sync is disabled in these local builds (syncEnabled=false with no env keys, syncService.ts:15). grep -n 'triggerSync\\|addEventListener' src shows no lifecycle hooks. Supabase postgres_changes are not replayed after a dropped socket, and iOS drops sockets of suspended PWAs. Retry delay = min(120000, 1500\*2^n), so after a long offline spell the first sync after reconnect can be up to 120 s away.
- **Verification:** impact: confirmed (P1); code: confirmed (P1); reproduce: confirmed (P1). Sources: PWA-06.

### F-019 · P1 · Data — Quick-add reads Singapore place and brand names as months: entries are saved back-dated, future-dated or without their amount

- **Where:** src/utils/parseExpense.ts:41-61 (month patterns use the prefix match `(jan\|...\|dec)\w*`; '<n> <month-prefix>\w\*' consumes amounts; the date is pulled out before the amount; the year defaults to the current year with no future-date guard), :21-22 (AMOUNT_RE takes the first number), :64-77; src/features/entry/SmartInput.tsx:53-60
- **Impact:** Wrong data is saved from the fastest one-hand entry path without the user noticing (the only cue is a small date pill and a toast prefix). Junction 8, Novena, Marina, Marine Parade, Decathlon, Sephora, Mayo and Octopus are everyday words for a Singapore user. Spend is filed in the wrong month or year (it never shows in the current month, which looks like data loss), and future dates are possible even though the full form caps the date at today.
- **Fix:** Match exact month tokens only (`\b(jan(uary)?\|feb(ruary)?\|...)\b`) directly adjacent to a day number, and never when it would consume the only number. Never resolve to a future date (roll back one year, or ignore); show the year in the pill when it differs; block future dates as the pickers do (max=today). Prefer the interpretation that leaves an amount. When the parsed date is not today, show the date pill in the accent colour as a warning. Add a table-driven corpus of ~50 SG place/brand inputs to parseExpense.test.ts, plus a property test that the amount is never lost when exactly one number is present.
- **Evidence:** [UXM-02] s04*smartinput.cjs typed each input into the real SmartInput and pressed Enter. 'junction 8 dinner 15' was SAVED as 'dinner' $15 dated 2026-06-08 (4 months back), category 'Bills'. 'grab novena 12' parsed to Nov 12 (future) and lost the amount. 'decathlon 25' parsed to Dec 25 (future, empty item). 'sephora 30' parsed to Sep 30 with no item and no amount. 'grab to marina 12' parsed to Mar 12 and lost the amount. Pills seen: ['dinner','$15.00','Bills','','Jun 8']. Screenshots: shots/smart*_*sephora_30.png, shots/smart*_\_grab_novena_12.png. [DAT-16] s5.log section 5: 'grab 18 junction 8' -> amount=8, date=2026-06-18. 'grab 12 marine parade' -> date 2026-03-12, amount lost. 'kopi 2 novena' -> future 2026-11-02. s7.log, typed on 2027-01-02: 'nye dinner 31 dec 85' -> stored 2027-12-31, a year in the future, pill shows only 'Dec 31'. 'grab last fri 14' on a Saturday -> 8 days ago, not yesterday. [TST-06] Real Quick add bar, WebKit iPhone 13 on :4173 (TQ/quickadd-parse.cjs/.log, TQ/quickadd-junction_8_lunch_12.png): 'junction 8 lunch 12' stored as itemName 'lunch', amount 12, date 2026-06-08. 'decathlon 30' gives amount null and Add stays disabled. Unit probe: 'novena 5' -> Nov 5 with no amount; 'wet market 5' -> item 'wet', Mar 5, no amount; 'coffee 4.50 31 dec' -> future 2026-12-31; '2 coffee 3.50' -> amount 2; 'laptop 1,200' and 'S$5 chicken rice' -> no amount. parseExpense.test.ts has only 3 happy-path tests.
- **Verification:** code: confirmed (P1); reproduce: confirmed (P1); impact: confirmed (P1). Sources: UXM-02, DAT-16, TST-06.

### F-020 · P1 · Data — 'Week' and the insight windows depend on the time of day: before noon they drop today's entries and add an 8th day, and three screens disagree on 'this week'

- **Where:** src/features/ledger/LedgerScreen.tsx:116-129 (filter start=subDays(now,7), end=now at :119-121) vs the label at :224-227 (subDays(now,6)); src/lib/analytics.ts:308-332 (7 calendar days); src/utils/insights.ts:33-37, :43-46, :74-75 (rolling window [now-7d, now) compared with noon-stored rows), :62, :125 (hard-coded $); noon-stamped dates from AddExpenseSheet.tsx:80, EditExpenseSheet.tsx:71 and SmartInput parsed dates, while SmartInput undated entries store the real time (useExpenseStore.ts:194)
- **Impact:** Every morning, anything logged today through the full form (or edited today) disappears from the Week view and its total while an extra day leaks in, week-over-week insights understate spend, and narrative insights change through the day. Ledger, Insights and Pulse show different numbers for 'this week', which undermines trust in every total.
- **Fix:** One src/domain/dates.ts with dayKey(date) and periodRange(kind, now) on whole calendar days with inclusive ends: start = startOfDay(subDays(now, 6)), end = endOfDay(now); previous week = the 7 calendar days before. Use it in Ledger, Pulse, Insights and insights.ts, and format insight amounts through the money module. Store SmartInput entries at local noon like every other path so one date contract holds. Add fixed-clock tests at 09:00 and 13:00.
- **Evidence:** [UXM-03] s05_week.cjs, nine noon-stamped expenses, page clock 2026-10-18 (SGT). At 09:00 the view shows 'day-1-ago'..'day-7-ago': it includes Oct 11, outside the label 'Oct 12 - Oct 18, 2026', and excludes today. At 15:00 it shows 'day-0-ago'..'day-6-ago'. Both read '7 entries' with different totals. Screenshots: shots/week_0900.png, shots/week_1500.png. [ARC-09] Chromium, clock Sun 18 Oct 2026 09:00 SGT; $10 today (local noon), $7 on 11 Oct, $20 on 4 Oct. Ledger 'Week' shows $7.00 listing only the 11 Oct entry under 'Oct 12 - Oct 18, 2026'; Insights 'This week' shows $10.00 and Pulse 'Today $10.00' (scratch/out/browser-checks.log weekSemanticsInBrowser, shots/ledger-week-0900.png). Root causes: 3 separate period implementations plus mixed stored date forms (owner backup: 353 date-only + 288 ISO-Z dates). [DAT-12] Real browser at 09:00 SGT (<scratch>/b4-misc.cjs, b4.log 3): range=today shows 'Breakfast today', range=week does NOT ('shows Breakfast? false'). Screenshots b4-ledger-today-0900.png, b4-ledger-week-0900.png. s5.log section 4: Ledger week filter keeps only 't2' and drops today's $50; computeWeekAnalytics includes it; the insight says 'up 100% this week' when, with today's spend, it is 600%. [TST-14] TQ/probe/logic.probe.test.ts: the same 3 rows give 'Everything looks on track' at 09:00 and 'Food & Dining up 200% this week' at 13:00, because a row logged this morning is stored at 12:00 local and is 'in the future' until noon. Month-pace with Sep 15 $100 and Sep 30 $500 shows 'last month $100' instead of $600. Amounts always shown with '$'.
- **Verification:** code: confirmed (P1); reproduce: confirmed (P1); impact: confirmed (P1). Sources: UXM-03, ARC-09, DAT-12, TST-14.

### F-021 · P1 · Data — 'vs last month' pill compares month-to-date with ALL of last month, showing a misleading green 'down' most of every month (year view likewise)

- **Where:** src/lib/analytics.ts:95-115 (prevStart..prevEnd = full previous month), :197-199 (changePct = (periodTotal - prevTotal) / prevTotal), :240-249, :295 (year: YTD vs the full prior year); src/features/pulse/PulseScreen.tsx:131-137
- **Impact:** The most prominent trend signal on the home screen reads green 'down' almost every day until month end (about -97% on the 1st) and reassures the user while they may be overspending - the worst failure for a budget app.
- **Fix:** Compare like with like: the previous month up to the same day-of-month (and the previous year up to the same day-of-year), or the full-month projection against last month's total. Say which in the label ('+41% vs Sep 1-18' / 'vs same point in Sep').
- **Evidence:** [UXD-02] Demo on 18 Oct: Oct 1-18 = $708.80 and Sep full month = $997.87 (year sparkline label 'Peak $997.87 on Sep'). 708.80/997.87-1 = -29.0%, exactly the green pill in shots/p4174-1440x900-dark-pulse.png. Year view does the same (year to date vs the full prior year). [DAT-19] On day 18 of 31, a user spending at exactly last month's rate sees roughly '-42% vs last month' (18/31 of the total); the pill says 'vs last month' with no qualifier. [PRD-02] Run on the demo data, Oct 18 (parse-entry.ts -> parse-entry.out.json): pill -29.0% (green, down arrow; demo-pulse-sgd-phone.png). Previous month to the same day was S$502.67, so like-for-like spending is UP 41.0%. Projected month-end S$1,220.71 is 22.3% ABOVE September's S$997.87. With steady S$40/day the pill would read -90.0% on day 3. Insights 'Pacing vs last month' also uses lastMonthEnd = new Date(y, m, 0) (00:00 on the last day), dropping noon-stored final-day expenses.
- **Verification:** code: confirmed (P1); reproduce: confirmed (P1); impact: confirmed (P1). Sources: UXD-02, DAT-19, PRD-02.

### F-022 · P1 · Data — Insight 'Pacing vs last month' drops the last day of last month and can report the opposite direction

- **Where:** src/utils/insights.ts:115-117 (lastMonthEnd = new Date(y, m, 0) = 00:00 of the last day, compared with d < end); hard-coded $ at :61, :92, :130
- **Impact:** Spending booked on the last day of a month (often rent, bills, month-end payments) disappears from the comparison, so the Insights screen shows a wildly wrong or inverted spend-pace headline.
- **Fix:** lastMonthEnd = endOfMonth(subMonths(now, 1)) with an inclusive comparison, via the shared period module (or reuse computeMonthAnalytics' prevTotal). Format amounts through the money module.
- **Evidence:** [ARC-10] Clock pinned to 18 Oct 2026; data Sep 10 $50 + Sep 30 $100 + Oct 5 $60. Insight shown: 'Pacing 107% higher than last month', detail 'Projected $103 · last month $50'. True last month is $150, so pace is actually 31% lower (scratch/out/date-tests.log insights_monthPace). [DAT-13] Pure run frozen at 2026-10-20 SGT (s5.log section 3) with Sep 10 $100 and Sep 30 $900: computeMonthAnalytics prevTotal = 1000 (correct), while computeInsights says 'Pacing 830% higher than last month \| Projected $930 · last month $100'.
- **Verification:** code: confirmed (P1); reproduce: confirmed (P1); impact: confirmed (P1). Sources: ARC-10, DAT-13.

### F-024 · P1 · Perf — Pulse recomputes dead O(365 x N) date-parsing analytics on open, tab return and every add/sync: multi-second main-thread freezes at the owner's ~640 entries

- **Where:** src/lib/analytics.ts:180-187 (month daily: 30 x expenses.filter(isSameDay(parseISO))), :189-191 + :281-283 (recentEntries, full sort with parseISO, never read), :227/:288 (refDate + void refDate), :270-279 (YearAnalytics.daily: 365 x filter(parseISO), never read), :308-319 (week 14 x N); src/features/pulse/PulseScreen.tsx:47-54 (month AND year analytics memoised on [expenses, budgets], computed on every mount regardless of view)
- **Impact:** The core daily loop (open app, log, see the total) freezes for over a second on a laptop and about 6 s on a phone-class CPU at the current ~640 entries; taps are dropped. Cost grows linearly (~8-10 s at 1k entries, reached around May 2027), and every chevron tap, Year tap, tab return and sync round repeats it.
- **Fix:** (1) Delete YearAnalytics.daily and both recentEntries (dead) and refDate. (2) Build one memoized per-expenses-array index (WeakMap<Expense[], {t: Float64Array, dayKey: string[], byDay: Map}>) using new Date(iso) or local construction for 'yyyy-MM-dd' instead of parseISO in hot loops; derive month daily, heatmap, week buckets and period totals from byDay in O(N + days) (see the equivalence-checked PoC in the scratch folder). (3) Compute year analytics only when view==='year'. (4) Optionally lift analytics into a store-level memo so remounting Pulse is free. Add a correctness test for computeYearAnalytics and a vitest perf guard (compute\* on 5k rows < 50 ms).
- **Evidence:** [ARC-05] Chromium, prod build :4173, ~640 seeded expenses (the owner's 2026-09-20 backup holds ~640): Pulse load long task 1,307 ms; quick-add Enter -> toast 1,266 ms with one 1,195 ms long task. 4x CPU throttle: 7,950 ms on load, 10,178 ms per add. 2,000 expenses: 3,492 ms per add on desktop CPU (scratch/out/browser-perf.log, shots/pulse-_.png). Node: computeYearAnalytics 1,460 ms at ~640 and 3,622 ms at 2,000 vs computeMonthAnalytics 146 / 460 ms (out/perf-tests.log). `.daily` is only read from monthData (PulseScreen.tsx:209-250); yearData.daily and both recentEntries have no readers. applySync always builds a new expenses array, so every sync round repeats the freeze. [PRF-01] CPU profile at ~640 (out/profile-pulse-~640-x1.json): top self time all date-fns parseISO internals (new RegExp per call), constructFrom, isSameDay, startOfDay. Pulse load-to-content at 4x (out/routes-_.json): empty 0.80-1.04 s; ~640 5.7-14.0 s (one long task 5.1-11.5 s); 1k 8.3-10.3 s; 5k 38.9-46.5 s. Unthrottled desktop ~640: 0.88-1.62 s vs 0.18-0.33 s empty. WebKit iPhone 13 profile: 1.42-1.64 s vs 0.34-0.40 s. SmartInput add at 4x: 6.9 s at ~640, 10.2 s at 1k. Returning to Pulse from Insights at 4x: 7.0-15.0 s long task (Pulse unmounts, useMemo lost). Node: computeYearAnalytics 0.94-2.15 s at ~640, 7.0-12.8 s at 5k. A PoC day-bucket index (fast-analytics.ts) returns identical daily/week output in 1.0 ms at ~640 and 5.3 ms at 5k (~900-1300x faster). [TST-18] node/vitest (TQ/perf-probe.log): year 696 ms at 500 rows, 1388 ms at 1000, 2799 ms at 2000; month 58 / 149 / 252 ms. No test for computeYearAnalytics.
- **Verification:** code: confirmed (P1); impact: confirmed (P1); reproduce: confirmed (P1). Sources: ARC-05, PRF-01, TST-18.

### F-025 · P1 · Product — Pace ring is unlabeled and measures all spending against only the budgeted categories ('81%' reads as budget used; a single budget gives a false red 148%)

- **Where:** src/lib/analytics.ts:126-128; src/features/pulse/PaceRing.tsx:3-17; src/ui/Ring.tsx:21-26; src/features/pulse/PulseScreen.tsx:272-305
- **Impact:** The most prominent secondary metric on the home screen invites the wrong reading on every visit, and people who budget only one or two categories get a false red alarm. There is no 'left to spend' number to anchor it.
- **Fix:** Compute pace over budgeted categories only, or against an optional explicit overall monthly budget. Label the ring ('On pace 81%' with '47% of $1,500 used'), add a tooltip and sr-only text, or switch to %-of-budget with a pace tick. Show 'S$791 left · S$57/day' beside it, and lay it out beside the total on mobile instead of in its own row.
- **Evidence:** [PRD-03] totalBudget is the sum of the category caps, but periodTotal includes every category. In walk3 the only budget was Food & Dining at S$300, 3% used, yet the ring showed a RED 148% (migration-pulse.png) because unbudgeted Investment, Travel and other spending counted against that S$300. In the demo the ring shows '81%' with only '13d left' while 47% of the S$1,500 total budget is spent (parse-entry.out.json); 81% is a pace ratio but nothing says so. [UXM-24] shots/cur_SGD_pulse.png: '81%' and '13d left' centred below the sparkline, taking about 110px of the first viewport, with no label saying what is at 81%. [UXD-23] Demo: $708.80 of a $1,500 total budget = 47% used; the ring shows '81%' (spend ÷ expected by day 18) with no title or aria-label (shots/p4174-1440x900-dark-pulse.png).
- **Verification:** code: confirmed (P1); reproduce: confirmed (P1); impact: confirmed (P1). Sources: PRD-03, UXM-24, UXD-23.

### F-026 · P1 · Product — Settings copy misstates where data lives when sync is on: 'no cloud, no sharing across devices', a 'locally stored' Clear-all that wipes every linked device, Disconnect, offline banner and iOS-only install text

- **Where:** src/features/settings/SettingsDrawer.tsx:429-459 (Backup copy; :434-436 'No account, no cloud, no sharing across devices automatically'; :447-450 iOS-only install steps; :455-456), :513-516 (Clear-all confirm 'permanently removes all locally stored expense data ... budgets ... are kept'); src/features/settings/SyncPanel.tsx:153-226, :225-227, :351-353 (Disconnect); src/app/AppShell.tsx:158-160, :164-171 (offline banner); src/stores/useExpenseStore.ts:123-141, :267-274; docs/USER_GUIDE.md:52
- **Impact:** A user who believes 'local only' wipes phone and laptop at once with Clear all, and gets a wrong picture of where his financial data lives and what Disconnect and Clear do, on exactly the screen he would open when worried about losing data. Android/desktop users get iOS-only install steps; the offline banner promises a sync that is not configured.
- **Fix:** Make all copy depend on sync state. With a syncId: 'Synced through your relay; every linked device has a full copy; backups are an extra safety net'; Clear-all says 'This deletes N expenses on all linked devices', requires typing DELETE and takes an automatic local snapshot first; Your data stays in the relay and on your other devices'; offline banner says 'will sync when back online' only when sync is configured. Show platform-correct install steps (beforeinstallprompt Install button on Chromium). Store lastBackupAt and show 'Last backup N days ago'. Update USER_GUIDE.md.
- **Evidence:** [DAT-14] The Backup panel never checks syncEnabled/syncId, but clearAllExpenses tombstones every id and those tombstones propagate to every device (screenshot <scratch>/b4-clear-all-confirm.png). Demo build :4174 (b4.log 4): 59 -> clear -> 0 -> reload -> 59 again, and the user's budget {'Food & Dining':999} is replaced by the demo budgets, contradicting 'budgets ... are kept'. [SEC-08] The Backup tab always says data lives only in this browser and tells the user to move data with JSON export/import, even with sync connected. Disconnect says 'other devices stop syncing', but they keep syncing and the data stays in Supabase as plain JSON. The sync warning understates relay exposure; Clear-all says 'locally stored'. [UXM-18] s10_settings.cjs backup.copy: 'No account, no cloud, no sharing across devices automatically' and 'Multiple devices: Export a JSON backup here, then import it on the other device.' while the Sync tab offers a sync code and cross-device linking. [UXD-32] Same copy next to the Sync tab with sync ON in production (shots/settings-dark-Backup.png). [PWA-14] offline.log: the banner 'changes will sync when connection is restored' renders on the :4173 build where syncEnabled=false and no syncId exists; no beforeinstallprompt handling in src (grep). [PRD-11] walk1.log.json shows the same Backup text; import 'will replace all current data', a risky path to recommend with sync on; grep finds 0 calls to navigator.storage.persist().
- **Verification:** code: confirmed (P1); reproduce: confirmed (P1); impact: confirmed (P1). Sources: DAT-14, SEC-08, UXM-18, UXD-32, PWA-14, PRD-11.

### F-028 · P1 · PWA — After every deploy, stale lazy chunks crash Ledger/Insights to the 'Unexpected Application Error! Hey developer' page, and the 'App updated, reload' toast dismisses itself instantly

- **Where:** vite.config.js:51-55 (workbox skipWaiting + clientsClaim + cleanupOutdatedCaches); src/app/routes.tsx:6-11 (lazy routes), :31-59 (no errorElement); vercel.json:2 (catch-all rewrite turns a missing chunk into index.html 200); src/app/AppShell.tsx:113-127 (update toast with duration: 0 at :118-120); src/ui/ToastHost.tsx:19-24 (duration ?? 4000, then setTimeout(dismiss, duration)); dist/registerSW.js (register only, no registration.update())
- **Impact:** On the first launch after most releases the whole UI (tab bar included) is replaced by a developer error page; an installed iOS PWA has no reload control, so the user must force-quit. The one prompt meant to prevent this is never visible, and a phone resumed from background never checks for updates, so it can sit on a stale build for days. During the overhaul's many deploys this will hit Shade repeatedly.
- **Fix:** (1) In ToastItem treat duration 0 (or null) as sticky (skip the timer). (2) Switch to a prompt-style update: registerType 'prompt' without skipWaiting, so the old SW keeps serving the old page's chunks until the user accepts; drive the prompt from the waiting worker (hand-rolled updatefound/waiting -> postMessage(SKIP_WAITING) -> reload, ~1 KB; avoid virtual:pwa-register autoUpdate, which reloads mid-form). Defer the reload while an entry/edit sheet is dirty. (3) Wrap lazy() imports in a one-shot reload-on-chunk-failure helper guarded by sessionStorage, and add a root errorElement with a 'Reload' button. (4) Exclude /assets/ from the vercel.json rewrite so a missing chunk returns 404. (5) Call registration.update() on visibilitychange to visible, throttled to about once an hour. Keep sw-update.cjs as a regression test; the deploy carrying the fix can itself trigger one last crash.
- **Evidence:** [ARC-04] Aborting the LedgerScreen-\*.js request and tapping Ledger renders 'Unexpected Application Error! Failed to fetch dynamically imported module ... 💿 Hey developer 👋' (scratch/shots/chunk-load-failure.png). Rendering the real ToastHost in jsdom with the exact AppShell update toast ({duration: 0, action Reload}): already gone at the first flush, while a default toast survives (scratch/harness/toast-test.tsx, out/toast-test.log). [PWA-02] Simulated deploy with Vercel-like serving (pwa-platform/srv.cjs, make-v2.cjs re-hashes chunks), then sw-update.cjs: the cold launch gets the OLD shell from precache (index-yU4QgA3X.js); the new SW claims the page 2.2 s later (Chromium) / 1.15 s (WebKit) and drops LedgerScreen-DkDPc62T.js from precache. Tapping Ledger requests the old chunk, the server rewrites it to HTML, and the page shows 'Unexpected Application Error! Failed to fetch dynamically imported module ... Hey developer' (update-deploy.log, shots/update-deploy-2-ledger.png). Same for Insights (update-deploy-insights.log) and in WebKit as "'text/html' is not a valid JavaScript MIME type" (update-deploy-webkit.log, shots/update-deploy-2-Ledger-webkit.png). Control run renders fine (update-control.log). 71 first-parent commits on main since 2026-04-17. [PWA-03] pwa-platform/sw-toast.cjs -> sw-toast.log: controllerchange at 1992 ms, toast text appears at 2005 ms and is gone at 2482 ms (exit animation only); toastNodesAfter7s=0; sw-update.cjs could not find the toast at all (toastText=null). grep for registration.update / visibilitychange in src: none.
- **Verification:** code: confirmed (P1); reproduce: confirmed (P1); impact: confirmed (P1). Sources: ARC-04, PWA-02, PWA-03.

### G-008 · P1 · Data — Pulse, Ledger and Insights never notice that the day, month or year has changed. They keep showing yesterday's 'Today' numbers, last month's budget and stale insights for days

- **Where:** src/features/pulse/PulseScreen.tsx:47-54; src/lib/analytics.ts:88-98,122-124,161-163,179,225-252; src/features/ledger/LedgerScreen.tsx:105-143; src/features/insights/InsightsScreen.tsx:47-54; src/utils/insights.ts:31; nothing in src listens for visibilitychange, pageshow or focus (only useExpenseStore.ts:485-488 pagehide/beforeunload/online/offline)
- **Impact:** The owner checks spending each morning on an installed PWA or a laptop tab left open overnight. Both resume from memory, so he reads yesterday as 'Today', last month as the current month, and over-budget alerts for a month that has ended. The numbers do not fix themselves until he switches tabs or logs something, and then they jump without explanation.
- **Fix:** Add a single app clock: a tiny store `useClock` holding `today` (yyyy-MM-dd). Update it from a timeout re-armed for the next local midnight + 1 s, and from visibilitychange (visible), pageshow and focus, setting state only when the key changes. Make computeMonthAnalytics, computeYearAnalytics, computeWeekAnalytics, computeInsights and forecastDailyFromRecurring take `now: Date` as a parameter. Add `today` to every memo dependency list (PulseScreen, LedgerScreen filtered/dateRangeLabel, InsightsScreen) and build `now` inside the memo. Cost is about 0.5 KB. Add a page.clock e2e that crosses 23:59->00:01 and 31 Dec->1 Jan.
- **Evidence:** The probes ran on Chromium (390x844 mobile) and WebKit (iPhone 13) with TZ Asia/Singapore and page.clock. The clock moved across 23:59 -> 00:01 while the page was visible, then to 07:30, then through resume events (visibility hidden->visible, pagehide, pageshow persisted, focus, online), then +72 h. Results are in s1*pulse_rollover.json, s2_ledger_rollover.json and s3_insights_rollover.json, with screenshots in shots/s1*_, s2\__ and s3\_\*. Pulse loaded at Sat 31 Oct 23:59 shows 'October 2026 $497.00 · Today $42.00 · Avg $16.03/day', pace ring '90% 0d left', budget 'Transportation Over by $45.00 · 0d left in October', Today strip '2 entries $42.00' and heatmap footer 'Jul 26 – Oct 31, 2026'. At Sun 1 Nov 00:01, at 07:30 after the resume events, and on Wed 4 Nov 07:30 (3 days later, still mounted), every one of those values is identical, on both engines. The year-end case is the same: 'December 2026 $467.00 · Today $42.00 · 0d left in December' is still on screen on Mon 4 Jan 2027. A Ledger->Pulse remount gives 'November 2026 $0.00 · Today $0.00 · 26d left in November'. Ledger with ?range=today stays at '$42.00 · 2 entries' after midnight, and Month and Year likewise keep their pre-midnight totals until some filter dependency changes. Insights still says 'Food & Dining is over budget · $302 of $300 · 0d left in October' and 'This week $145.00' on Wed 4 Nov. After a remount it shows 'Transportation down 47% this week' and 'This week $71.00'. Stale values end only on a remount (tab switch) or a data change. When a data change ends them, the screen jumps: after 'coffee 4 yesterday' (an Oct 31 entry) the Pulse hero went from 'October 2026 $497.00' to 'November 2026 $0.00' (s4_arrows_and_add.json add-refresh).
- **Verification:** code: confirmed (P1); reproduce: confirmed (P1); impact: confirmed (P1). Sources: GAP2-01.

### F-007 · P2 · A11y — Sheets move focus to Close on open, overriding the autoFocus field (Ctrl+N then typing logs nothing), and drop focus to <body> on close

- **Where:** src/ui/Sheet.tsx:25-34 (setTimeout focuses closeButtonRef, overriding autoFocus), :71, :73; src/features/entry/AddExpenseSheet.tsx:162; src/features/ledger/EditExpenseSheet.tsx:91
- **Impact:** Laptop logging by keyboard (Ctrl+N, type, Enter) is broken: keystrokes are lost and Enter dismisses the form, and every desktop add costs 6 extra keystrokes. After every add or edit, keyboard and screen-reader users are thrown to the top of the document; VoiceOver users on the phone lose their place after every log.
- **Fix:** In Sheet, capture the return target before children mount (store document.activeElement in a ref during render when open goes false to true), or take returnFocusRef/initialFocusRef props. Only focus Close when !panelRef.current.contains(document.activeElement), so autoFocus wins; otherwise focus the first [autofocus]/input in the panel. On cleanup, return focus to the captured trigger only if it is still connected, else to a sensible fallback (FAB or ledger list). Put the What field before the date pills in tab order, or focus it directly.
- **Evidence:** [A11Y-01] React runs the child Input's autoFocus during commit, before Sheet's useEffect. Sheet.tsx:27-28 then records document.activeElement (the sheet's own 'What'/'Item' input) as previousFocus, and :32-34 moves focus to Close. Measured in Chromium and WebKit (iPhone 13): focus after open = 'Close' in Add, Edit and Full-entry sheets (kbd-results.json K3, webkit-results.json). Desktop: Ctrl+N, type 'Coffee', press Enter: the What field stays '' and Enter closes the sheet (misc-results-all.json ctrln: focusAfterCtrlN='Close', typedCoffeeLandedInWhatField='', afterEnter_sheetOpen=0). On close by Escape, Close, Cancel or Save, focus goes to the hidden sheet input and ends on BODY about 500 ms later, for all 7 trigger/close combinations tested (focusreturn-results.json focusLog 'out BUTTON[Close] -> INPUT ... out INPUT -> null'). Settings has no autoFocus field and returns focus correctly, which confirms the root cause. [UXD-04] 05a-keyboard-flows-AB.log B1: 'after Ctrl+N focus = BUTTON[Close] in:Add expense'; typing 'Laksa' right after Ctrl+N went nowhere. B3: it takes 6 Tabs (Today, Yesterday, 2d, 3d, Pick date) to reach 'What'. 07 L6: Enter on a ledger row opens Edit with focus on Close.
- **Verification:** code: confirmed (P1); reproduce: confirmed (P2); impact: confirmed (P2). Sources: A11Y-01, UXD-04.

### F-008 · P2 · A11y — Date picker cannot be operated by keyboard or screen reader, and Escape while it is open closes the whole Add/Edit sheet and discards the entry

- **Where:** src/ui/DatePicker.tsx:118-132, :163-268 (portal to document.body, focus never moved, no arrow keys), :274-290; src/ui/Sheet.tsx:36-40, :42-63; src/app/AppShell.tsx:138-143
- **Impact:** Keyboard and VoiceOver/TalkBack users cannot backdate an expense more than 3 days, change an expense's date in Edit, or pick a custom Ledger range; because aria-modal hides content outside the dialog, screen readers do not see the calendar at all. Pressing Escape to dismiss the calendar silently discards everything typed in the Add form.
- **Fix:** Render the popover inside the sheet (pass panelRef as the createPortal container) or register it with the trap. On open, focus the selected or today cell with a roving tabindex and Arrow/Home/End/PageUp/PageDown navigation, role=grid with aria-selected. Handle Escape in DatePicker in the capture phase with stopPropagation, close only the popover and return focus to the trigger; have Sheet/global Escape skip events already handled. Cheaper path: back the pill with a native <input type='date'> and call showPicker() for the OS picker plus free keyboard/screen-reader support.
- **Evidence:** [A11Y-02] The calendar is portaled to document.body, outside the aria-modal='true' sheet (insideSheet=false, sheetAriaModal='true'). Focus stays on the trigger when it opens; the Sheet's Tab trap only cycles panelRef, so 12 Tab presses never reach the calendar (kbd-results.json K6 everReachedCalendar=false). DatePicker has no keydown handling, and Escape is caught by Sheet's window listener, which closes the entire Add sheet while the calendar is open (afterEscape sheetStillOpen=0). On Ledger 'Custom' range it took 187 Tab presses to reach the calendar, and Escape left it open (K6b). [UXD-06] 06-escape-picker-currency.log E2: filled 'Hawker dinner' + 6.5, opened 'Pick date', pressed Escape: dialogs [] and on reopen the fields are empty. E3d: on Ledger Custom, Escape does not close the picker at all. [UXD-07] 05a log B4/B5: Enter on 'Pick date' opens the popover but focus stays on the trigger; Tab moves to What/Amount while the popover stays open outside the Sheet's focus trap. 06 log E3e: on Ledger custom range the popover was not reached within 60 Tabs. B6: ArrowDown does nothing.
- **Verification:** reproduce: confirmed (P1); code: confirmed (P1); impact: confirmed (P2). Sources: A11Y-02, UXD-06, UXD-07.

### F-009 · P2 · A11y — Focus indicator is effectively invisible: ~1.2:1 in light, ~1.66:1 in dark, absent in forced-colors mode, and identical to the selected style

- **Where:** src/index.css:205-222 (button/a/input/select/textarea:focus-visible uses box-shadow 0 0 0 4px var(--accent-ring) and outline: none at :213-218); src/index.css:32 (--accent-ring 25% alpha), :138 (30% alpha light)
- **Impact:** A keyboard user on the laptop cannot see where focus is: WCAG 2.4.7 fails in light theme, and 1.4.11 / 2.4.13 fail in both themes. Combined with the focus jumps elsewhere, users cannot tell that focus moved. Windows High Contrast users get no focus indicator on most controls.
- **Fix:** Replace the box-shadow ring with a real outline token: --focus-ring (accent #22d3ee in dark, #0e7490 in light) at 3:1 or better per theme, applied as outline: 2px solid var(--focus-ring); outline-offset: 2px on :focus-visible, so focus differs from selected. Remove the 'outline: none' override at index.css:213-218; outlines survive forced-colors mode. Keep the inner 2px surface-0 halo for separation if wanted.
- **Evidence:** [A11Y-03] Pixel diff of focused vs blurred screenshots for 272 tab stops across Pulse, Ledger, the Add sheet and Settings, both themes, 1280 and 375 widths (focusvis-results.json): 250 of 272 (92%) have no pixel whose colour changes by 3:1 or more. Light theme: 130 of 136 have none, max ~1.2:1. Dark: max ~1.65-1.8:1. Only Button.tsx components (ring-accent/50) reach ~3.4:1. Crops: shots/focus-today-chip-light.png, shots/focus-navlink-dark.png. With forced-colors: active, chips, inputs and selects report outline-style none and box-shadow none (forced.cjs, shots/forced-colors-addsheet.png). [UXD-19] 13-focus-contrast.log: effective ring rgb(18,61,67) on #0c0b0a = 1.66:1, rgb(185,237,243) on #faf8f5 = 1.20:1, against the WCAG 1.4.11 minimum of 3:1. shots/focus-ring-dark.png, shots/focus-ring-light.png: the focused 'Month' segment looks the same as the selected one.
- **Verification:** code: confirmed (P2); impact: confirmed (P2); reproduce: confirmed (P2). Sources: A11Y-03, UXD-19.

### F-013 · P2 · Data — Tombstone cap of 1,000 prunes by insertion order: clear-all of >1,000 entries (or a long-offline device) brings deleted expenses back

- **Where:** src/services/syncService.ts:19 (TOMBSTONE_CAP=1000), :155 (`[...tombstones].slice(-TOMBSTONE_CAP)` keeps the most recently inserted ids, not the newest by time); src/stores/useExpenseStore.ts:267-274 (clearAll adds every id)
- **Impact:** Deleted or cleared spending reappears and inflates every total. Old tombstones age out by count, so a long-offline device resurrects old deletions. Likelihood grows with history size.
