- **Fix:** Store tombstones as {id: deletedAt} and prune by age (e.g. older than 180 days, and only once every device has synced since), or keep them indefinitely; never prune by insertion position. Use a clearedAt watermark for clear-all. Add tests for both.
- **Evidence:** [DAT-04] Real store + real mergeBackups (<scratch>/harness/s4-merge-semantics.cjs, s4.log C4): clear-all on A with 1,500 expenses -> cloud keeps 1,000 tombstones -> device B (still holding 1,500) merges and keeps 500 -> A's next sync gets '500 expenses back on the device that cleared'. The same happens to any device offline across more than 1,000 deletions. Tombstones are ~22 B each, so the cap buys almost nothing. [TST-07] TQ/probe/sync.probe.test.ts: 1500 rows, clearAllExpenses on A, merge and push: remote ends with 0 expenses and 1000 tombstones; device B keeps 500 after its sync and pushes them back (TQ/probe-run.log). Shade's 2026-09-20 backup is 171 KB, which puts the ledger in the ~1000-row range (size only).
- **Verification:** code: confirmed (P1); reproduce: confirmed (P1); impact: confirmed (P2). Sources: DAT-04, TST-07.

### F-014 · P2 · Data — A corrupt or unparseable storage key boots an empty app and deletes the only copy; the next entry then wipes the whole history

- **Where:** src/services/storage.ts:61-80 (corrupt JSON -> removeItem(KEY_EXPENSES) at :76 after the trailing-comma repair fails; a non-array silently becomes []; :66 rows parsed as Expense[] with no per-row check), same pattern at :92-93, :106-107, :120-121, :130-138 (getBudgets returns a parsed `null`), :134-135, :148-149, :162-163, :178-179; src/stores/useExpenseStore.ts:153-156 (catch -> hydrated:true with empty defaults)
- **Impact:** Low likelihood (needs a torn write, a buggy migration or a manual edit), but the blast radius is the entire history with no warning: the app looks like a fresh install and the first tap destroys the evidence. Without sync there is no recovery, and wrong-shape rows feed the launch crash.
- **Fix:** On any read or parse failure, copy the raw value to '<key>.corrupt.<timestamp>' with safeSet instead of removeItem, enter a recovery mode that blocks writes, and show a banner/toast offering 'Download raw data' and 'Reset'. Optionally salvage records up to the last complete object. Validate and repair per record (same sanitizer as the import/sync validator) instead of throwing, and guard each getter's shape (object/array) separately so one bad key cannot block the others. Add tests.
- **Evidence:** [DAT-09] Real browser (<scratch>/b2-corrupt-keys.cjs, b2.log): truncated JSON in 'expenses' -> 'KEY DELETED', app shows $0.00. Object instead of array -> $0.00, then one quick-add stores only [water] (2 entries gone). One null element -> hydrate throws -> empty app -> after one add 'expenses' = [water]. categoryBudgets='null' -> hydrate throws on Object.keys(null) -> the EXPENSES key is overwritten after one add (a corrupt budgets key destroys expense data). categoryMappings='null' -> every add throws silently. [TST-17] Probe: a truncated payload makes getExpenses() return [] and leaves localStorage 'expenses' null, with no other key holding the raw data. [{id:1, amount:'12', date:'garbage'}, null] is returned as 2 rows (TQ/probe-run.log). [PWA-09] corrupt.cjs -> corrupt.log: storing a 42,236-char expenses blob truncated by 37 chars (299 recoverable records), then loading /ledger, leaves localStorage.getItem('expenses') = null.
- **Verification:** code: confirmed (P1); impact: confirmed (P2); reproduce: confirmed (P1). Sources: DAT-09, TST-17, PWA-09.

### F-017 · P2 · Data — The last expense is not saved when the app is backgrounded: the 400 ms save debounce is flushed only on pagehide/beforeunload, never on visibilitychange

- **Where:** src/stores/useExpenseStore.ts:453-459 (400 ms debounce), :485-486 (flush only on pagehide/beforeunload)
- **Impact:** Narrow-window silent data loss for a log-then-pocket user: add an expense, swipe home or lock within about 0.4 s, and if the OS later evicts the suspended PWA the expense never existed. The pill showed 'Saving…' at the time. With sync on, the 3 s push is stranded too (see PWA-06).
- **Fix:** Add a visibilitychange listener: when the page turns hidden, call flushPendingSave(), and also fire any pending sync push (see PWA-06). Keep pagehide for bfcache. Optionally write on add/delete with no debounce (the measured write cost is 6.9 ms at ~640 expenses, see measurements).
- **Evidence:** pwa-platform/lifecycle-flush.cjs -> lifecycle-flush.log, deterministic in both engines: quick-add, then dispatch visibilitychange(hidden) in the same task, and the expense is NOT in localStorage (persistedAtHideInstant=false in Chromium and WebKit). With pagehide it is (true). lifecycle-trace.cjs -> lifecycle-trace.log: killing the renderer (CDP Page.crash, i.e. a process kill with no unload events) about 220 ms after Enter left no setItem('expenses') call, and the expense was lost (survived=false). Mobile OSes fire visibilitychange on app switch, not pagehide, then suspend the page and may kill it later.
- **Verification:** code: confirmed (P1); impact: confirmed (P2); reproduce: confirmed (P2). Sources: PWA-05.

### F-023 · P2 · Data — Year view shows two contradictory 'monthly average' figures on the same screen

- **Where:** src/features/pulse/PulseScreen.tsx:179-184 vs src/features/pulse/MonthlyBars.tsx:15,109
- **Impact:** Contradictory numbers on the overview screen undermine trust in every figure.
- **Fix:** Pick one definition (months elapsed for a budget view), compute it once in analytics and reuse it in both places. Name it explicitly if both are kept.
- **Evidence:** shots/pulse-year-1440.png: hero 'Monthly avg $200.93' ($2,009.34 / 10 months elapsed) and Monthly breakdown 'Avg/mo $669.78' ($2,009.34 / 3 months with spending), about 300px apart.
- **Verification:** reproduce: confirmed (P2); code: confirmed (P2); impact: confirmed (P2). Sources: UXD-03.

### F-027 · P2 · Product — Demo build re-seeds 59 fake expenses (and demo budgets) whenever the store is empty: after Clear all + reload, and with no sync guard

- **Where:** src/stores/useExpenseStore.ts:123-141 (seed whenever IS_DEMO && expenses.length === 0, no syncId check); src/app/AppShell.tsx:154-163 (demo banner); README.md:82-84; docs/brain/OPERATING-MANUAL.md ('Deployed: https://spendtrack-demo.vercel.app')
- **Impact:** Anyone following the documented path gets sample spending (and budgets) mixed back into their real ledger; with sync on, the resurrected demo-N ids sit alongside their own tombstones and connecting a fresh device can push 59 fake Singapore expenses into the real synced dataset. If Shade's daily driver is spendtrack-demo.vercel.app, this applies to him.
- **Fix:** Seed only once ever (persist a demoSeeded flag, or seed only when no storage keys exist), or only on an explicit 'Try demo' action, and never when a syncId exists. Prefix demo ids with 'demo-' and exclude them from sync. Clearing should leave demo mode and hide the banner. Confirm which VITE_DEMO_MODE value each deployment uses.
- **Evidence:** [PRD-04] walk2.cjs on :4174 (walk2.log.json): 59 expenses before, 0 after Clear all, 59 after reload, while deletedExpenseIds holds those same 59 ids. The README tells phone users to install the demo ('Open the demo on your phone -> Add to Home Screen') and to 'make it yours' with Clear all; the banner repeats it. Screenshot: demo-after-clear-reload.png. [ARC-22] hydrate seeds 59 DEMO_EXPENSES whenever IS_DEMO && expenses.length === 0 without checking for a syncId; setupSync then merges local into remote (useSyncStore.ts:129-143). After 'Clear all', a reload seeds the demo data again. The Vercel environment was not visible, so whether the owner's daily URL is a demo build is unverified.
- **Verification:** code: confirmed (P1); impact: confirmed (P2); reproduce: confirmed (P1). Sources: PRD-04, ARC-22.

### F-029 · P2 · PWA — Installed iPhone PWA: the top bar draws under the status bar / Dynamic Island (safe-area-inset-top never applied)

- **Where:** index.html:8 (viewport-fit=cover), index.html:18 (apple-mobile-web-app-status-bar-style black-translucent); src/index.css:104 (--safe-top defined, unused); tailwind.config.js:76 (spacing 'safe-top', unused); src/app/TopBar.tsx:20-21 (sticky top-0, py-3, no safe-top padding); src/app/AppShell.tsx:154-163 (demo/offline banners)
- **Impact:** On an iPhone with the app on the home screen, the logo, palette button and settings button overlap the clock, battery and Dynamic Island; taps there hit iOS's scroll-to-top instead of the buttons. The first thing seen on every launch looks broken. Android is not affected.
- **Fix:** Add padding-top: env(safe-area-inset-top) to the sticky header (e.g. pt-safe-top on the header wrapper, keeping the 12 px inner padding), or to the first top element (demo/offline banner when present); use the same inset in the top offset of any fixed overlay. Set the status-bar style per theme, or 'default' for light. Verify with visual.cjs (inset override works in Chromium) and on a device in standalone mode.
- **Evidence:** [UXM-04] grep for safe-area-inset-top / --safe-top across src/ and index.html finds only the unused variable definition. With black-translucent plus viewport-fit=cover in standalone mode, iOS lays content from y=0 under a 47-59 pt status bar. TopBar content sits at y≈12-52 CSS px (sweep geometry: Settings 40x40, palette 39x40 at the top). The Backup tab tells users to 'Add to Home Screen' (SettingsDrawer.tsx:448-450). [PWA-04] pwa-platform/visual.cjs with CDP Emulation.setSafeAreaInsetsOverride top=47 (iPhone 13) on :4173: env(safe-area-inset-top)=47 px while headerTop=0 and logoTop=18 (visual.log); the 40 px command/settings buttons span y 12-52, so 35 px sit in the status bar zone. Screenshots shots/safearea-dark.png, shots/safearea-light.png show the logo colliding with the '9:41' clock. In light theme black-translucent forces white status text over the #faf8f5 header, so clock and battery become invisible. Bottom inset is handled (tab bar padding-bottom 34 px).
- **Verification:** reproduce: confirmed (P2); impact: confirmed (P2); code: confirmed (P1). Sources: UXM-04, PWA-04.

### F-030 · P2 · UX — Any parent re-render while a Sheet is open re-runs its focus effect and yanks focus to Close: keystrokes are lost mid-typing and a typed space closes the add sheet

- **Where:** src/ui/Sheet.tsx:25-73 (effect deps [open, onClose]; each re-run focuses Close at :32-34); inline onClose closures at src/app/AppShell.tsx:218, :226, src/features/ledger/LedgerScreen.tsx:450, src/features/settings/SettingsDrawer.tsx:58-63 (:60), src/features/entry/SmartInput.tsx:185; re-render triggers: AppShell.tsx:76 (undoStack subscription), :68 (isOffline), SmartInput.tsx:76-79 (clearUndo at 6 s)
- **Impact:** The most common daily sequence (a quick add, then a full-form add within 6 s) breaks: on a phone the keyboard drops mid-word, on a laptop a space discards the form. Patchy connectivity (online/offline flips) and any sync pull that changes expenses while the Edit sheet is open set off the same thing, and every settings change sends keyboard and screen-reader users back to the top of the drawer.
- **Fix:** Keep onClose in a ref (useLatest: onCloseRef.current = onClose) and make the Sheet effect depend on [open] only, so a re-render cannot run the cleanup and focus code again; this protects every caller. Also wrap the onClose handlers in useCallback in AppShell, LedgerScreen and SettingsDrawer, and apply the same pattern in CommandPalette. On open, focus the first field instead of Close.
- **Evidence:** [UXM-01] s11_focus_steal.cjs (WebKit + Chromium): quick-add 'coffee 4.50', open FAB sheet, type in What. At t=6102 ms (WebKit) / 6172 ms (Chromium) focus jumps from What to the Close button and the next keystrokes are lost (What = 'chicken ri'). s11b: typing ' rice' after the jump makes the space press Close, so the sheet closes and the form is gone (dialogOpen=false). Going offline while typing the amount also moves focus from Amount to Close. Screenshots: shots/focus_steal_webkit.png, shots/focus_steal_space.png. [ARC-15] Chromium at 1280 px, Settings open, focus on #currency-select -> selectOption('EUR') -> document.activeElement = 'Close'. Clicking the 'Budgets' tab -> activeElement = 'Close' (scratch/out/browser-checks.log sheetFocusSteal). [A11Y-04] kbd-results.json K4: focus 'Budgets' tab, press Enter -> focus lands on 'Close'; same after Space on the 'Light' theme radio and after ArrowDown on the currency select. Each re-render creates a new onClose, so cleanup refocuses the trigger, the effect re-arms and setTimeout focuses Close again; body overflow toggles each time.
- **Verification:** reproduce: confirmed (P1); code: confirmed (P1); impact: confirmed (P2). Sources: UXM-01, ARC-15, A11Y-04.

### F-031 · P2 · UX — Insights '14-day forecast' chart always renders empty (bars are 0px tall) at every viewport

- **Where:** src/features/insights/InsightsScreen.tsx:220-233 (percentage heights inside flex columns with no definite height); compare the Week chart at :135-170
- **Impact:** A headline Insights card looks broken on every visit on desktop, tablet and phone, and the upcoming recurring charges it should show are invisible.
- **Fix:** Make each column wrapper `h-full` (or drop `items-end` on the row and use `justify-end` inside each column) so the percentages resolve, or compute pixel heights from a fixed BAR_H the way the Week chart does. Add day ticks for days with a projected charge.
- **Evidence:** [UXM-11] s14_forecast.cjs, WebKit and Chromium: 14 bars with inline heights ['2%',…,'100%','2%'] all render at 0px, and their columns are also 0px. The card shows a total of $25.97 over a blank chart in both themes (shots/demo_iphone13_dark_insights.png, shots/demo_iphone13_light_insights.png). [UXD-01] 02-charts.json: at 1440/1280/1024/768/375 all 14 bars measure 0px tall although style heights are '2%'..'100%' and the header shows $25.97. Cause: row is `flex h-20 items-end`, each column wrapper `flex flex-1 flex-col` gets content height (items-end, no stretch), so the inner `flex-1` box is 0px and %-heights resolve to 0. Screenshots shots/forecast-1440.png, shots/p4174-1440x900-dark-insights.png.
- **Verification:** code: confirmed (P1); reproduce: confirmed (P1); impact: confirmed (P2). Sources: UXM-11, UXD-01.

### F-032 · P2 · A11y — Core touch targets under 44px across every screen and sheet, and text inputs only ~25px tall inside their 44px boxes (against the project's 44px rule)

- **Where:** src/features/entry/SmartInput.tsx:117-124 (14x14 clear), :127-149 (pen 32x32, Add 62x31); src/features/pulse/PulseScreen.tsx:92-114 (period chevrons 24x24, 2 px apart); src/ui/Segmented.tsx:45 (sm 28px tall); src/ui/Sheet.tsx:122-130 (Close 32x32); AddExpenseSheet/EditExpenseSheet category chips (29px); src/ui/DatePicker.tsx:175-194, 227 (nav 28x28, days 32x36); src/features/ledger/LedgerScreen.tsx:263-271 (CSV 32x32), :389-395 ('Clear all filters' 71x17); TodayStrip 'View' 42x19; Heatmap cells 17x17; SettingsDrawer tabs 81x29, category delete 28x28, budget inputs 32px; src/ui/Input.tsx:30-49 (44 px visual field, input ~25 px tall); src/ui/ToastHost.tsx:43-59; src/features/ledger/LedgerRow.tsx:144-154 (32x32 delete beside the amount)
- **Impact:** These are the controls for daily, one-handed logging and filtering (Add, presets, chips, chevrons): missed taps and accidental deletes, and tapping the visible field edge without focusing the input feels like the app ignored the tap. The project's anti-pattern doc bans targets under 44px.
- **Fix:** Make the whole Input wrapper a <label> (or forward clicks / give the input h-full). Raise Segmented sm to h-9 with a >=44px hit area (pseudo-element padding), SmartInput Add/pen/clear and Sheet Close to 44px, chips to py-2 (>=36px visual with a 44px hit area), date grid rows to 44px, period arrows to 40px with 8px spacing. For dense visuals (heatmap) keep the visual size and enlarge the hit area with an invisible overlay. Move row Delete behind the swipe or into the Edit sheet.
- **Evidence:** [UXM-15] s01_sweep.cjs smallTargets() at 390/375/320 on every route and open sheet: demo iPhone 13 Pulse 109 sub-44 targets (98 heatmap cells), Ledger 42 (29 row trash buttons), Add sheet 12, Edit sheet 18, Settings/Categories 20. s06: date-picker day cells 32x36 and month nav 28x28. Raw lists in s01_sweep.json. [A11Y-18] Measured at 375 px (evidence-results.json): text inputs are 25 px tall inside a 44 px bordered box, so a tap on the box's top or bottom 9 px does nothing (What, Amount, Notes, Search). SmartInput clear 14x14, toast Dismiss 14x14, Undo 29x16. Period prev/next 24x24. Segmented options and chips 28-30 px tall. Row Delete 32x32 next to the amount. docs/brain/qa-checklist.md:60 requires 'Tap targets >= 44x44 px on mobile'; apart from the heatmap, axe passes these only via the 2.5.8 spacing exception.
- **Verification:** combined: confirmed (P2). Sources: UXM-15, A11Y-18.

### F-033 · P2 · A11y — Ledger rows: buttons nested inside role=button, plus an invisible focusable swipe-Delete per row (4 tab stops and 2 'Delete' buttons per row)

- **Where:** src/features/ledger/LedgerRow.tsx:63-72 (swipe button, opacity 0, always focusable), :73-92 (div role=button tabIndex=0), :89-91, :119-154 (Edit/Delete buttons inside it, named just 'Edit'/'Delete')
- **Impact:** Reaching the 20th row takes about 80 Tabs and a quarter of the stops are invisible; keyboard users tab onto an invisible delete before every row. Screen readers hear two delete buttons per row with no item context, and some AT (iOS VoiceOver) flatten and hide the inner Delete because children of role=button are presentational.
- **Fix:** Make each row an <li> with one real <button> for edit and one delete button named `Delete ${itemName}`; do not nest buttons in a role=button container. Use one tab stop per row with a roving tabindex (ArrowUp/Down, Enter=edit, Delete=delete with undo). Give the swipe-revealed button tabIndex={revealed ? 0 : -1} and aria-hidden={!revealed}, or drop it in favour of a single mobile delete.
- **Evidence:** [UXD-13] 04-tabstops.log: /ledger has 133 tab stops for 29 rows, with 87 focusables inside opacity-0 ancestors. 03-tab-order: the 'Delete <item>' swipe button gets focus at opacity 0. 09-axe-overlays.json: nested-interactive (serious) x29 in both themes. Hover buttons are labelled just 'Edit'/'Delete'. [A11Y-06] axe nested-interactive (WCAG 4.1.2, serious) on 29 nodes on /ledger and 59 on the custom range, every theme and viewport, and in WebKit (axe-results.json, webkit-results.json). Desktop /ledger 133 stops, 87 with opacity 0 at focus time; 29 are swipe buttons covered by the row (kbd-results.json K1_desktop_ledger, K1m_mobile_ledger invisible=29). CDP AX tree: row exposed as button 'Edit Kopi O at kopitiam, $1.20, Food & Dining' with a child button 'Delete', and 29 identical 'Delete' names (misc-results-all.json axtree_ledger).
- **Verification:** combined: confirmed (P2). Sources: UXD-13, A11Y-06.

### F-034 · P2 · A11y — Activity heatmap: 92 tab stops between Top categories and the budget section, sub-24px targets, and no-op buttons for zero-spend days

- **Where:** src/features/pulse/Heatmap.tsx:94-109 (one <button> per day), :100 (click ignored when total is 0), :102 (h-[17px] w-[17px] sm:h-[19px] with 3 px gap)
- **Impact:** Keyboard users must press Tab about 92-100 times to reach Budget vs actual or the Today strip. One-hand thumb taps on 17 px cells hit the neighbouring day, and screen-reader users hear 98 'No spending' buttons.
- **Fix:** Make the heatmap a single tab stop: role='grid' (or a group) with a roving tabindex; arrows move by day/week and Enter opens the ledger. Render zero-spend days as non-interactive cells. Use at least 20 px cells with a 4 px gap (24 px centre spacing) on mobile. Add a skip link and a one-line text summary (e.g. 'Busiest day: Tue Sep 22, $120').
- **Evidence:** [UXD-14] 04-tabstops.log: /pulse has 120 tab stops, 92 of them heatmap day buttons (including no-spend days that do nothing on click). 03-tab-order stops 18-45+. [A11Y-07] axe target-size (WCAG 2.5.8 AA, serious) on 92 nodes on every Pulse state in both themes, both widths and WebKit: '19px by 19px, safe clickable space diameter 20px' (axe-results.json). Desktop Pulse 120 stops, 92 (77%) heatmap cells; mobile Pulse 122 stops (kbd-results.json K1).
- **Verification:** combined: confirmed (P2). Sources: UXD-14, A11Y-07.

### F-035 · P2 · A11y — Command palette: focus escapes the aria-modal dialog, and arrow-key selection is invisible to assistive tech (no combobox/listbox semantics)

- **Where:** src/features/entry/CommandPalette.tsx:178-191 (ArrowUp/Down change visual activeIdx only), :205-245 (dialog; input without role/aria-\* at :218-227), :252-311 (option buttons)
- **Impact:** Keyboard users can lose focus behind the overlay. A screen-reader user pressing Enter runs an action they never heard, which could be Export or the quick-add of the typed text.
- **Fix:** Apply the ARIA 1.2 combobox pattern: input role='combobox' with aria-expanded, aria-controls=listId, aria-activedescendant=optionId and an aria-label; results in role='listbox' with role='option', ids and aria-selected; section labels as group labels. Give options tabIndex=-1 so Tab cycles only input and Clear, or trap Tab with a shared dialog primitive.
- **Evidence:** [UXD-08] 05a log A2: from the palette input, Tab goes through 6 actions, then BODY, then the header links behind the aria-modal dialog. A3: listbox=false, options=0, aria-activedescendant=null, input role=null, aria-label=null (placeholder only). [A11Y-09] kbd-results.json K7: input role=null, aria-controls=null, aria-activedescendant=null. After 2 ArrowDown presses the visually active item is 'Go to Ledger', but aria-selected count is 0 and listbox/option count is 0. 14 Tab presses left the palette into 'SpendTrack', 'Pulse', 'Ledger', 'Insights' and 'Quick add expense' behind the backdrop. Escape returns focus to the trigger correctly.
- **Verification:** combined: confirmed (P2). Sources: UXD-08, A11Y-09.

### F-040 · P2 · A11y — Save and sync status is not reliably announced: the SyncPill live region is remounted on every change and every tick, and is hidden below 1280 px

- **Where:** src/app/SyncPill.tsx:55-60 (key={label + tick} on the role=status node), :23-26 (15 s tick); src/app/TopBar.tsx:50-52 (hidden xl:block); src/app/AppShell.tsx:164-171 (offline banner, no live role); src/features/settings/SyncPanel.tsx:252-263 (cloud status, no live role)
- **Impact:** With cross-device sync on in production, a failed save or sync gives no audible signal. On the phone there is no save or sync status at all, apart from the offline banner.
- **Fix:** Keep one persistent role='status' node and change only its text when the state changes. Do not put the relative time in the announced text: render it aria-hidden, or as a separate non-live span. Use role='alert' for 'Save failed' and 'Sync failed'. Put role='status' on the offline banner. Show a compact status on mobile.
- **Evidence:** A MutationObserver on the header recorded the role=status element being removed and re-inserted 4 times in 15 s after one quick-add: 'Saving…' at 1765 ms, 'Saved · just now' at 2085 ms, 'Saved · 5s ago' at 7681 ms, 'Saved · 13s ago' at 15129 ms (misc-results-all.json syncpill). Screen readers generally do not announce content of a newly inserted live region, so 'Save failed' may never be spoken. If they do announce it, they are told the elapsed time every 15 s. At widths below 1280 (every phone) the pill is display:none, and the offline banner and cloud 'Sync failed' text are not live regions.
- **Verification:** combined: confirmed (P2). Sources: A11Y-15.

### F-043 · P2 · Code — App bootstrap, persistence and sync wiring are scattered across a view, a store and a mutable module flag; names mislead and settings bypass safeSet

- **Where:** src/app/AppShell.tsx:29-64 (bootstrap in a component; a second subscribe() watching 5 fields vs persistence watching 7 at useExpenseStore.ts:443-471); src/services/syncService.ts:23 (exported mutable syncApplying); src/stores/useExpenseStore.ts:19,40-42 (SyncStatus = local save state; isOffline), :21-26 (undo closures stored in state); src/app/SyncPill.tsx (shows local save state, hidden below xl per TopBar.tsx:50); src/stores/useUIStore.ts:18, :95, :101 (raw localStorage.setItem)
- **Impact:** Every sync fix has to touch four places. The misleading names have already produced a 'sync' pill that shows save status. Theme and currency changes are lost silently when storage is full, and rule violations go unnoticed.
- **Fix:** Add app/bootstrap.ts, called from main.tsx (hydrate, persistence, sync engine, SW-update listener). data/persistence.ts owns the subscribe; sync/engine.ts owns push scheduling and the applying flag. Rename syncStatus -> saveStatus and SyncPill -> SaveStatus. Route settings storage through storage.ts (a safeSet-backed setPref) with validated reads, and add a no-restricted-properties lint rule for localStorage.setItem outside storage.ts. Store undo entries as serialisable commands.
- **Evidence:** [ARC-20] Sync behaviour spans useSyncStore -> useExpenseStore.getState(), AppShell's subscription, syncService's flag and SyncPanel. `syncStatus` in the expense store is the localStorage save status, while `cloudStatus` is the actual sync status. useUIStore calls localStorage.setItem directly, which breaks the safeSet rule in OPERATING-MANUAL, and casts any stored theme string to Theme without checking it. [TST-22] grep: useUIStore.ts:95 and :101 are the only two raw setItem calls outside storage.ts. They sit in a try/catch with an empty catch, so a full-storage error is swallowed silently instead of raising the quota event.
- **Verification:** combined: confirmed (P2). Sources: ARC-20, TST-22.

### F-044 · P2 · Code — Three divergent add-expense pipelines and two different fuzzy category matchers

- **Where:** src/features/entry/SmartInput.tsx:53-82, AddExpenseSheet.tsx:70-101, CommandPalette.tsx:76-98; src/utils/parseExpense.ts:103-111 (Fuse threshold 0.4) vs src/stores/useExpenseStore.ts:159-175 (threshold 0.3 + score cutoff 0.4); undo lookup by id string in SmartInput.tsx:68-78, AddExpenseSheet.tsx:89-96, LedgerScreen.tsx:169-172
- **Impact:** The same text gets a different category depending on which entry surface is used, and every behaviour fix has to be made three times, so the copies drift.
- **Fix:** One useAddExpense() hook (parse -> validate -> add -> haptic -> toast with Undo) used by all three surfaces. One domain smartCategory() with a Fuse index memoised on the mappings reference. A store.undo(entryId) API instead of matching id-prefix strings.
- **Evidence:** With the same mappings, 'grab home' gets Transportation from the quick bar but is filed as Other by the full form (scratch/out/domain-tests.log dualFuzzyMatchers). The palette path has no Undo and hard-codes '$'. Only SmartInput schedules clearUndo (6 s). Quick-add without a date stores the current instant, while the forms store local noon. A new Fuse index is built on every keystroke (parseExpense: 1.2 ms at 350 mappings, 4.6 ms at 1,500) and on every add (getSmartCategory: 1.8 / 6.9 ms) (out/perf-tests.log).
- **Verification:** combined: confirmed (P2). Sources: ARC-17.

### F-045 · P2 · Code — Light theme works by overriding literal utility class names, so new classes silently break it (e.g. invisible year bars)

- **Where:** src/index.css:1197-1380 ([data-theme='light'] .text-white, .border-white\/\[0\.06\], …); src/features/pulse/MonthlyBars.tsx:67-70; LedgerScreen.tsx:436, TodayStrip.tsx:39
- **Impact:** Every new opacity value is a light-mode bug waiting to happen, and index.css has grown to 1,393 lines, much of it overrides.
- **Fix:** Bind semantic Tailwind colours to CSS variables defined per theme (fg, fg-muted, line, line-strong, fill-muted, bar). Run a codemod to replace the white-alpha utilities, then delete the override block. This keeps the v0.8.3 warm palette and only changes how it is wired.
- **Evidence:** 120 text-white uses plus 76 white-alpha border/bg uses in TSX depend on per-class light overrides. 6 of the 28 distinct white-based utilities have none: divide-white/[0.04] x3, bg-white/30, bg-white/[0.05], bg-white/[0.15], hover:border-white/[0.14], hover:border-white/10 (scratch/out/theme-overrides.log). In light mode the Year 'Monthly breakdown' bars are rgba(255,255,255,0.15) on rgb(255,253,251), so the Aug and Sep bars are invisible (shots/light-monthly-bars.png, out/light-bars.log).
- **Verification:** combined: confirmed (P2). Sources: ARC-19.

### F-046 · P2 · Data — Quick-add parser takes the first number, rejects 'S$5' / thousands commas, folds category, currency and date words into the item name, and has no outlier guard

- **Where:** src/utils/parseExpense.ts:22 (AMOUNT_RE takes the first standalone number, `\$?` only), :25-61, :90 (leftover words become the item), :94-111 (category only fuzzy-matched from item-name mappings); docs/USER_GUIDE.md:9-22
- **Impact:** Natural Singapore phrasing, including the User Guide's own examples, produces wrong amounts, dates or names from the fastest entry path, and Shade has to open Edit to fix them. A fat-fingered extra zero corrupts monthly totals unless he catches the 4 s toast.
- **Fix:** Prefer the last number or a number with decimals when several appear. Accept S$/SGD/$ prefixes and suffixes and thousands commas. Treat a trailing word matching a category name or alias as the category. Parse ISO and d/m dates and bare weekday names. Strip 'monthly'/'weekly' tokens (later connect them to recurring). Show a confirm pill for amounts above ~10x the user's median. Correct USER_GUIDE. Write the failing tests first.
- **Evidence:** [UXM-13] s04_smartinput.cjs: 'mala 2 pax 18' saved as 'mala pax 18' $2.00. '2 coffee' saved $2. 'chicken rice S$5' gave 'add amount' (no amount found). 'lunch 8.5 food' saved item 'lunch food', category Other. 'coffee 4.5 sgd' saved item 'coffee sgd'. 'coffee 99999999' was accepted and saved as $99,999,999.00 with only a toast. 'coffee 4,50' gave 'add amount' with no hint why. [PRD-08] parse-entry.out.json, 19 realistic inputs, five misread silently: 'netflix 15 monthly' -> item 'netflix monthly' (no recurring support despite USER_GUIDE.md:12,21); 'lunch 8.90 2026-05-01' -> item 'lunch 2026-05-01' dated today (USER_GUIDE.md:13); '2 kopi 3.40' -> $2.00 'kopi 3.40'; 'mcd 2 pc meal 8.90' -> $2.00; 'bubble tea 6.5 sat' -> item 'bubble tea sat', dated today. Unparseable: 'S$12 grab', 'grab 1,200', 'shopee 12,50', 'grab 12.555'.
- **Verification:** combined: confirmed (P2). Sources: UXM-13, PRD-08.

### F-050 · P2 · Data — All data (and the only sync key) lives in best-effort, evictable localStorage: persist() is never requested and backups are never tracked or nudged

- **Where:** src/services/storage.ts (localStorage only, syncId at :206-216 in the same store); no navigator.storage usage anywhere in src; SettingsDrawer.tsx:344-347 (export with no lastBackupAt)
- **Impact:** Under storage pressure, browsers evict whole best-effort origins, localStorage included. Safari (tab, not installed) deletes script-writable storage after 7 days of Safari use without visiting the site. Either way the syncId is wiped with the data, so recovery needs the code from another device, and the prominent 'Generate sync code' button would start a new, empty space. Sync is not a backup either: Clear-all writes tombstones that propagate to every device (useExpenseStore.ts:267-273 plus the tombstone union in mergeBackups).
- **Fix:** Call navigator.storage.persist() after the first successful add, once per install. Chromium grants it silently for installed or engaged sites, and WebKit 17+ supports it. Record lastBackupAt on export and show 'Last backup N days ago' plus the persisted status in Settings, then Backup. Nudge after 30 days. Show the sync code in Settings with a copy button and warn before 'Generate' when a code may already exist.
- **Evidence:** platform-probe.cjs -> platform-probe.log: Chromium navigator.storage.persisted()=false (quota 6145 MB). grep 'navigator.storage' in src: none. Owner backup, aggregates only (backup-aggregate.log): ~640 expenses over 406 days, 114,320 B of expenses plus 12,237 B of mappings, all in one origin. The newest backup file in the repo is dated 2026-09-20: at 1.58 expenses/day, about 44 expenses since then exist only in localStorage plus the cloud row.
- **Verification:** combined: confirmed (P2). Sources: PWA-07.

### F-052 · P2 · Perf — Insights blocks 3-5 s on a phone-class CPU at ~640-1k entries: recurring detection is O(K^2) Fuse, on top of the O(30N)/O(14N) analytics

- **Where:** src/utils/recurring.ts:36-54 (Fuse over all unique names, then fuse.search for every key); src/features/insights/InsightsScreen.tsx:47-54; src/lib/analytics.ts:180-187, :308-319
- **Impact:** Insights becomes a multi-second freeze, and the cost grows quadratically with the number of unique merchants. Once PRF-01 is fixed, recurring detection is about 85% of what remains.
- **Fix:** After the PRF-01 index lands: (1) block the fuzzy pass, comparing only keys that share the first token or first 3 chars, and skip keys already claimed (this cuts O(K^2) to roughly O(K\*b)). (2) Only fuzzy-merge buckets whose combined size could reach 3. (3) Memoize detectRecurring by expenses identity in a module-level WeakMap so tab revisits are free. (4) If still above about 50 ms at 5k, run it in requestIdleCallback and show the card skeleton.
- **Evidence:** Chromium 4x, Insights load-to-content: empty 1.26 s; ~640 3.07 s (2.06 s long task); 1k 3.9-4.5 s; 5k 11.3-12.5 s. Pulse->Insights tab switch at 4x: 4.7 s at ~640, 5.0 s at 1k. Node: detectRecurring 357 ms at ~640, 616 ms at 1k (643 unique names), 1,716 ms at 5k (2,175 unique names); computeMonthAnalytics 89-163 ms and computeWeekAnalytics 31-71 ms at ~640.
- **Verification:** combined: confirmed (P2). Sources: PRF-03.

### F-053 · P2 · Perf — Ledger search, delete and scroll stall: each keystroke re-parses every date and re-renders every un-memoized row with 4 framer-motion nodes and `layout` projection, then renders a second time when the URL updates

- **Where:** src/features/ledger/LedgerScreen.tsx:105-143 (filter re-parses all N dates with parseISO, sort comparator parses again), :199-208 (setSearchParams 200 ms after each keystroke causes a second full render), :39 (VIRTUALIZE_THRESHOLD 150), :417-447 (motion.section layout + motion.li layout on every row); src/features/ledger/LedgerRow.tsx:18-25, :53-67 (useMotionValue + 2 useTransform + drag + 3 motion elements per row, no React.memo; onEdit/onDelete are fresh closures each render)
- **Impact:** Search, the main way to find and fix a past entry, lags 0.25-0.4 s per character at today's size on a phone-class CPU and over 1 s at larger sizes. Deletes and swipes feel sticky.
- **Fix:** (1) Filter with the PRF-01 index (epoch t / dayKey per expense, no parseISO per keystroke); keep the store sorted (it already is: date desc) and drop the re-sort, or sort by numeric t. (2) Wrap the search in useDeferredValue and derive the filtered list from the deferred value; sync ?q= with replaceState-on-blur or a longer debounce, not every 200 ms. (3) React.memo(LedgerRow) with useCallback'd onEdit/onDelete. (4) Remove `layout` from motion.li/section (keep a cheap CSS fade-in for new rows). (5) Lower VIRTUALIZE_THRESHOLD to about 60, or always virtualize. (6) Cached formatter (PRF-05).
- **Evidence:** Event Timing per keystroke at 4x, month view (out/interactions-x4-_.json): ~640 entries / 14 rows p50 240 ms, max 264 ms; 1k / 24 rows 400/416 ms; 5k / 135 rows 496, 816, 1,568 ms. Range=All with only 10-15 rows virtualized still costs 256 / 288-424 / 432-1,664 ms, so the parse+filter+sort over N dominates there. Delete a row: 456 / 336 / 704 ms. Scrolling the 135-row month list at 5k: 25 long tasks totalling 4.7 s over 8 wheel ticks; trace shows Paint 526 ms + Layerize 361 ms of 1,670 ms main-thread time (out/trace-ledger-5000-x4.json). CPU profile at 5k shows formatMoney 5%, framer applyProjectionStyles / calcProjection / scrollLeft reads, and the parseISO family (out/profile-ledgerSearch-_.json). Heap after GC: 5.4 MB at ~640 vs 14.0 MB at 5k.
- **Verification:** combined: confirmed (P2). Sources: PRF-04.

### F-054 · P2 · Perf — formatMoney builds a new Intl.NumberFormat on every call (70x slower than a cached formatter) and is called hundreds of times per render

- **Where:** src/lib/format.ts:7-11, :17-19, :26-30; src/hooks/useFormatMoney.ts:4-7 (new function identity each render); callers: src/features/pulse/Heatmap.tsx:98-99 (2 calls x 98 cells), src/features/ledger/LedgerRow.tsx:91 + :115 (2 per row)
- **Impact:** This is a fixed tax of tens to about 100 ms per Pulse/Ledger render on a phone, paid on every keystroke and every data change. It is a cheap, safe win.
- **Fix:** Cache formatters in a module Map keyed by `${currency}\|${compact}`, and compute the currency symbol once per currency. Make useFormatMoney return a useCallback keyed on currency. In Heatmap, precompute labels once per data change with useMemo, using string slicing on 'yyyy-MM-dd' instead of parseISO.
- **Evidence:** Node: 1,000 formatMoney calls take 77-88 ms; 1,000 calls on a cached Intl.NumberFormat take 0.8-1.3 ms (bench-fns.out.txt). About 240 calls per Pulse render (Heatmap alone 196) means about 21 ms desktop / about 85 ms at 4x per render. About 290 calls per Ledger render at 135 rows. The 5k Ledger-search CPU profile shows formatMoney ('ky') as the top named JS function (208 ms self, 5%). Heatmap also calls parseISO 4x and format 2x per cell (Heatmap.tsx:91-99).
- **Verification:** combined: confirmed (P2). Sources: PRF-05.

### F-056 · P2 · Product — Smart categorisation starts empty on a real install and never learns from corrections

- **Where:** src/stores/useExpenseStore.ts:186-203 (learn only on add), 225-243 (updateExpense ignores mappings); src/demo/seedData.ts:131-166; src/features/entry/AddExpenseSheet.tsx:31-37,184; src/utils/insights.ts:97-107
- **Impact:** Most entries land in Other until the user hand-picks a category every time. Fixing them through Edit teaches nothing, so the burden never drops, which goes against the product promise of frictionless capture.
- **Fix:** Upsert the mapping in updateExpense when the category changes. Seed a small Singapore dictionary (the existing DEMO_MAPPINGS, about 1 KB) on first run. Pre-select the suggested chip live in the sheet. Make the Insights card link to /ledger?category=Other.
- **Evidence:** On the empty build (:4173, walk2.log.json), "grab 12.40" and "chicken rice" were both stored as "Other". The Singapore merchant dictionary (grab, mrt, ntuc, fairprice, shopee, watsons, singtel and others) exists only as DEMO_MAPPINGS and is used only in demo mode. In walk3, editing "Kit Kat" to Food & Dining left categoryMappings at {}. A fresh SmartInput "kit kat 1.50" then showed no category suggestion. The full Add sheet never previews or pre-selects the suggested category; it is applied invisibly at submit. Insights nags "N transactions need a category" using an all-time count, and the card cannot be tapped.
- **Verification:** combined: confirmed (P2). Sources: PRD-06.

### F-057 · P2 · Product — The default "Investment" category counts as spending, and no category can be excluded from totals or pace

- **Where:** src/constants.ts:3-15; src/lib/analytics.ts:107-115,240-249; src/utils/insights.ts:111
- **Impact:** Money moved into investments or savings, or transfers, makes a careful saver look like an overspender. It skews Top categories, month-over-month, pace and forecasts.
- **Fix:** Add a per-category "not spending" flag (for example excludedCategories, synced) and default Investment to excluded. Show it as a separate "Invested / set aside" line.
- **Evidence:** "Investment" is a default category, and every analytic sums every expense. In walk3, a S$150 "Prop firm evaluation" logged as Investment made up 58% of the month's "spending" and pushed the pace ring to a red 148% (migration-pulse.png).
- **Verification:** combined: confirmed (P2). Sources: PRD-09.

### F-059 · P2 · Quality — Quality gates are hollow: `npm run lint` lints 0 of the TS/TSX files and tsconfig switches off parts of strict mode; e2e and config TS are never type-checked

- **Where:** eslint.config.js:10 (files: ['**/*.{js,jsx}']; typescript-eslint not installed); tsconfig.json:15-17 (noImplicitAny false, noUnusedLocals false, noUnusedParameters false), tsconfig.json include ['src','vite.config.*'] (excludes e2e/, playwright.config.ts, vitest.config.ts); @types/node not installed; .github/workflows/ci.yml (Lint step)
- **Impact:** CI's Lint gate passes no matter what: hooks-order bugs, floating promises, unused code and JSX a11y misuse are never caught, the 'TS strict, no any' promise in CLAUDE.md and the 'no dead code' rule are not enforced, and e2e specs can drift without type errors. Today the gap hides no hooks bug, but nothing checks new code.
- **Fix:** Add typescript-eslint (strictTypeChecked with parserOptions.projectService) for \*_/_.{ts,tsx} with react-hooks, eslint-plugin-jsx-a11y, react-refresh, no-explicit-any, no-non-null-assertion, no-floating-promises and consistent-type-imports; set linterOptions.reportUnusedDisableDirectives='error' and run with --max-warnings 0. Turn on noImplicitAny (0 errors), noUnusedLocals/Parameters and noImplicitReturns now (6 one-line fixes), then noUncheckedIndexedAccess in a follow-up PR (42 sites). Add a tsconfig.e2e.json with @types/node and include it in npm run typecheck.
- **Evidence:** [ARC-13] `eslint -f json .` lints 6 files (eslint.config.js, postcss.config.js, scripts/_.mjs, tailwind.config.js, vite.config.js); for src/App.tsx it reports 'File ignored because no matching configuration'. The 2 eslint-disable comments in src have no effect. tsc errors per flag (scratch/tsc/out._.txt): noImplicitAny 0, noUnusedLocals 4, noUnusedParameters 1, noImplicitReturns 1, noPropertyAccessFromIndexSignature 6, exactOptionalPropertyTypes 13, noUncheckedIndexedAccess 42 (33 outside tests), noImplicitOverride 0. The repo's react-hooks rules over type-stripped src found only the 2 intentionally disabled deps (scratch/out/hooks-lint.log). [TST-09] npx eslint . -f json lints 6 files: eslint.config.js, postcss.config.js, scripts/check-bundle-size.mjs, scripts/smoke-preview.mjs, tailwind.config.js, vite.config.js (TQ/eslint.json). The eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps comments (AddExpenseSheet.tsx:54, LedgerScreen.tsx:207) suppress rules that never run. Type-stripped run of the hooks rules over all 61 files with a canary (TQ/lint-sim): 0 violations today. tsc --noUnusedLocals finds 5 dead symbols (Pill import, subDays, startOfWeek, nextDay, unused slice param). [TST-10] Same per-flag counts (TQ/tsc/out.\*.txt): noImplicitReturns at AppShell.tsx:84; noUncheckedIndexedAccess 42 (Sparkline 9, VirtualLedgerList 8, recurring 5, ...). tsc --listFilesOnly includes 0 e2e/config files; type-checking them fails only on missing Node types (Buffer, process).
- **Verification:** combined: confirmed (P2). Sources: ARC-13, TST-09, TST-10.

### F-060 · P2 · Quality — The riskiest stateful code has zero tests: sync merge (suite deleted and never restored), store actions, sync store, insight engine; ~89% of source untested and 0 of 9 recorded bugs have a regression test

The 16 deleted mergeBackups tests, recovered with git show af83b3e^:src/services/syncService.test.ts and run against current code, give 15 pass and 1 fail (version '3.0'->'4.0') (TQ/deleted-sync-tests.log). ### F-062 · P2 · Quality — The e2e suite is slow, flaky and mostly cannot fail; it also does not run in CI

- **Where:** e2e/routes.spec.ts:5-10 (waitForApp), :32, :62-63, :154, :164-165, :175 (crash assertions), :101-113 (add test), :138-155 (restore test), .github/workflows/ci.yml (no e2e step)
- **Impact:** The suite gives false confidence: the P0 crash in TST-02 would pass it. Because it takes minutes, nobody runs it.
- **Fix:** Rewrite on fixtures: seed localStorage with addInitScript, wait on a real readiness signal (e.g. a data-hydrated attribute), and replace fixed sleeps with expect.poll on localStorage. Add one shared assertion that the shell is present and no 'Unexpected Application Error' is shown. Cover the core journeys and run them in CI (see ideas).
- **Evidence:** waitForApp waits for every [aria-live=polite] node to detach, but ToastHost and SyncPill keep two such regions mounted, so it always hits the 8 s timeout and the .catch swallows it. Measured: 8011 ms against 342 ms for real readiness (TQ/measure-waitforapp.log). Full run: 28 tests, 414.7 s of summed test time (14.8 s average), 70 s wall time on 8 workers; CI uses workers:1, so roughly 7 minutes (TQ/e2e-run.log). The crash assertions look for /something went wrong/i, a string the app never renders; the router's default page says 'Unexpected Application Error!'. #root not.toBeEmpty also passes on the crash page from TST-02. The restore test picks a file but never clicks Import. '[mobile] submits a valid expense' failed 1 of 7 runs on WebKit (ledger showed '0 entries'): it relies on waitForTimeout(400), which races the 400 ms persist debounce (TQ/e2e-repeat.log). The Quick add bar, the main daily flow, plus edit, delete/undo, budgets, category rename, theme and offline have no e2e coverage.
- **Verification:** combined: confirmed (P2). Sources: TST-11.

### F-064 · P2 · Quality — CI gates are thin: Node 20 (end-of-life), a smoke test that never runs JS, and no e2e, a11y, coverage or audit checks

- **Where:** .github/workflows/ci.yml:27 (node-version 20), :31-52 (steps), scripts/smoke-preview.mjs:17-26, package.json scripts.a11y and format:check globs
- **Impact:** Regressions in behaviour, accessibility and dependencies reach production. The memory notes say red CI has been bypassed before.
- **Fix:** Move to Node 22/24 (matrix), add an e2e+axe job on seeded routes (with Playwright browser cache), vitest --coverage with thresholds, npm audit --audit-level=high, a TZ matrix, permissions: contents: read, and timeout-minutes. Replace the smoke test with a Playwright boot check that asserts the shell renders.
- **Evidence:** Node 20 reached end of life on 2026-04-30. The smoke test only checks that each of 4 routes returns 200 with 'SpendTrack' in the HTML; SPA fallback serves the same index.html for every path, so the render crash in TST-02 passes. There is no e2e, axe, coverage, npm audit or TZ step, and no permissions: or timeout-minutes. format:check covers only src/\*_ and root _.{js,json,md}, so e2e/, scripts/, root \*.ts and .github are never format-checked. npm run a11y audits only the empty '/' page. Playwright with axe-core on the seeded Ledger finds nested-interactive (serious, 29 nodes) in both themes (TQ/axe-baseline.json).
- **Verification:** combined: confirmed (P2). Sources: TST-19.

### F-065 · P2 · Quality — CRLF checkouts make `npm run format:check` fail locally, and there is no pre-commit hook to catch drift before CI

- **Where:** .gitattributes:1 (\* text=auto, no eol), system git config core.autocrlf=true (C:/Program Files/Git/etc/gitconfig), .prettierrc.json endOfLine lf; no .husky / lint-staged
- **Impact:** The local gate is red on a clean tree, so it gets ignored. Format drift has reached red CI twice (owner memory).
- **Fix:** Set \* text=auto eol=lf in .gitattributes and run git add --renormalize . Add husky + lint-staged (prettier --write and eslint --fix on staged files) and widen the format globs.
- **Evidence:** git ls-files --eol: 29 tracked files are i/lf but checked out CRLF, including package.json, index.html, e2e/routes.spec.ts, playwright.config.ts, vitest.config.ts, scripts/_.mjs and docs/brain/_ (TQ/git-eol.txt). Running prettier --check with the format:check globs exits 1 on package.json in an unmodified checkout (TQ/prettier-check.log).
- **Verification:** combined: confirmed (P2). Sources: TST-20.

### F-066 · P2 · Quality — The a11y gate scans 1 of about 21 UI states, so every issue above shipped green

- **Where:** package.json:48 ('axe http://localhost:4173 --exit'); docs/brain/qa-checklist.md:57-62
- **Impact:** The overhaul can regress a11y again without anyone noticing, since the checklist says '0 violations' while the real app has P1 keyboard failures.
- **Fix:** Replace the CLI call with a Playwright spec that seeds the demo data and runs axe on every route and overlay in both themes. Add assertions for initial focus, focus return, trap, Escape, and minimum focus-ring contrast. The scripts axe-scan.cjs, kbd.cjs, focusreturn.cjs and focusvis.cjs in this audit's scratch folder are ready seeds.
- **Evidence:** `npm run a11y` loads only :4173 (empty data, Pulse route, no overlay open). This audit's axe matrix of 21 states x 2 themes x 2 viewports (84 scans) found 9 rule ids: nested-interactive x29-59, select-name (critical), target-size x92, color-contrast, heading-order and more. Most of them only appear with data or with a sheet open (axe-summary.json). None of the keyboard defects (A11Y-01/02/04/09/10/11) are detectable by axe at all.
- **Verification:** combined: confirmed (P2). Sources: A11Y-19.

### F-068 · P2 · Security — Relay has no server-side limits on size, key format or shape, and the prune function can be executed by anyone

- **Where:** supabase/migrations/001_sync.sql:7-11,35-36; supabase/migrations/002_sync_indexes.sql:28-43 and 003_harden_prune_search_path.sql:10-23 (no REVOKE EXECUTE); src/services/syncService.ts:184-193 (1 MB check is client-only)
- **Impact:** Anyone can fill the free-tier database (500 MB). Supabase then puts the project into read-only mode and Shade's sync stops. A mass bucket wipe can also be possible. Local copies survive, since devices re-push, but new devices pair to an empty bucket until then.
- **Fix:** The SEC-01 RPC migration covers most of this. Also add to the table, cheap and independent: ALTER TABLE public.sync_buckets ADD CONSTRAINT sync_id_uuid CHECK (sync_id ~ '^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$'), ADD CONSTRAINT payload_size CHECK (pg_column_size(payload) <= 1048576), ADD CONSTRAINT payload_shape CHECK (jsonb_typeof(payload->'expenses') = 'array'); and run REVOKE DELETE, TRUNCATE, REFERENCES, TRIGGER ON public.sync_buckets FROM anon, authenticated. Schedule pruning with pg_cron as postgres instead of exposing it.
- **Evidence:** sync_id is an unconstrained TEXT primary key, and payload is JSONB with no CHECK. The only 1 MB guard is in client JS (syncService.ts:184-193), and anyone can bypass it with direct REST calls using the public anon key and the INSERT grant. Nothing limits row count, key format or payload shape. prune_stale_sync_buckets is SECURITY INVOKER and never has EXECUTE revoked. Postgres grants EXECUTE on new functions to PUBLIC by default, so anon can call /rest/v1/rpc/prune_stale_sync_buckets with stale_after '0'. That deletes every bucket if anon also holds DELETE. The migrations never revoke Supabase's default table privileges; I could not check this offline. Verify with: select grantee, privilege_type from information_schema.role_table_grants where table_name='sync_buckets'; and select has_function_privilege('anon','public.prune_stale_sync_buckets(interval)','execute');
- **Verification:** combined: confirmed (P2). Sources: SEC-06.

### F-070 · P2 · UX — Currency setting is ignored by the hero total and 12+ other surfaces, defaults to USD, is not synced, and SGD renders as 'SGD 14.60' next to '$708.80'

- **Where:** src/features/pulse/PulseScreen.tsx:120-129 (prefix='$', toLocaleString('en-US')); src/features/entry/SmartInput.tsx:160; src/features/entry/AddExpenseSheet.tsx:170; src/features/ledger/EditExpenseSheet.tsx:98; src/features/settings/SettingsDrawer.tsx:300; src/features/entry/CommandPalette.tsx:81, :94-96; src/features/insights/InsightsScreen.tsx:158; src/features/pulse/Sparkline.tsx:39, :123; src/utils/insights.ts:61, :92, :130; src/lib/format.ts:1-32 (en-US, new Intl.NumberFormat per call, min 2 decimals); src/stores/useUIStore.ts:37-43 (readCurrency falls back to 'USD'); src/stores/useExpenseStore.ts:373-386 (exportBackup omits currency/theme)
- **Impact:** Shade is in Singapore and the first run defaults to USD; after picking SGD the biggest number on the main screen (and what a screen reader speaks for the chart) shows a different currency from the rest of the screen, and 'SGD ' costs ~3 characters per amount on 320-390px rows. The project's own 'one currency util' rule is broken in 12 places, and the setting is lost on reinstall or a new device.
- **Fix:** Create domain/money.ts: cached Intl.NumberFormat per currency, currencyDisplay 'narrowSymbol' (or en-SG / the currency's home locale), currency-aware fraction digits (JPY 0), a space in the compact form. Route every amount through useFormatMoney / a <Money/> component (AnimatedNumber takes the formatter, insights.ts receives fmt, inputs and pills show the symbol, aria labels too) and ban `$${` via lint. Default currency from locale/time zone (Asia/Singapore -> SGD) or ask once on first run. Carry currency and theme in BackupData and sync.
- **Evidence:** [UXM-06] s08_currency.cjs with currency=SGD: pulseHero '$708.80' while the line below reads 'Today SGD 14.60 / Avg SGD 39.38/day' (shots/cur_SGD_pulse.png). Quick-add pill '$450.00', Amount prefix '$', budget prefix '$', insights tooltip '$29.99', insight copy 'vs last 7 days ($20 more)', palette 'Add "coffee" - $4.50'. With JPY: '¥14.60' (decimals) and the hero still '$708.80'. [UXD-22] 06 log E4 with SGD: hero '$708.80' next to 'SGD 14.60', 'SGD 39.38'; SmartInput pill '$1.80'; week tooltip '$87.15'; insight text '($20 more)'. JPY: '$708.80' vs '¥14.60'. Settings label promises 'SGD — S$' (shots/currency-SGD-pulse.png, shots/currency-SGD-insights.png). [ARC-12] Chromium :4174 SGD: hero '$708.80' with 'Today SGD 14.60' and top category 'SGD 174.50' on the same screen (scratch/out/browser-checks.log currency, shots/currency-sgd-hero.png). formatMoney(1234.5,'SGD') = 'SGD 1,234.50'; compact 'SGD12k'; JPY '¥1,500.00' (out/date-tests.log). 2,000 formatMoney calls 110 ms vs 1.3 ms cached (out/perf-tests.log). [DAT-18] b5.log with EUR on the demo build: Pulse shows 3 '$' amounts (incl. hero '$708.80') vs 32 '€'; Insights 4 '$' vs 7 '€'; stored expenses unchanged by the switch (display-only, as required). [A11Y-17] localStorage 'spendtrack:currency'='SGD': hero '$708.80' with 'Today SGD 14.60' under it; sparkline aria-label 'Total $1204.00'; Add sheet amount prefix '$' (final-checks.json currencySGD, addSheetAmountPrefix). [PRD-05] readCurrency() falls back to 'USD' and never reads navigator.language or the time zone (machine is Asia/Singapore, en-SG). Twelve call sites across nine files hard-code '$'. Insights narratives say '$20'/'$38' (walk1.log.json). demo-pulse-sgd-phone.png. exportBackup omits currency and theme, so a new device starts in USD again.
- **Verification:** combined: confirmed (P2). Sources: UXM-06, UXD-22, ARC-12, DAT-18, A11Y-17, PRD-05.

### F-071 · P2 · UX — Full-form entry fights the keyboard and one-hand use: focus lands on Close, Return on 'What' errors, the iOS decimal pad has no return key, and Add sits under the keyboard

- **Where:** src/ui/Sheet.tsx:32-35 (focus Close), :102-104 (fixed bottom-0, max-h 85dvh); src/features/entry/AddExpenseSheet.tsx:119-237 (:163 and :176 Enter calls submit; :166-177 type=number, inputMode=decimal, no enterKeyHint; :229-236 Add/Cancel at the end of a scrolling sheet); src/features/ledger/EditExpenseSheet.tsx:94-103 (same pattern); src/features/pulse/PulseScreen.tsx:65-67; index.html:8 (no interactive-widget); no visualViewport handling in src
- **Impact:** The fallback path for anything SmartInput cannot parse, used several times a day, is slow and error-prone one-handed: extra taps and reaches, the primary button hidden whenever the user is typing, and desktop Ctrl+N input silently dropped.
- **Fix:** Focus Amount (or What) on open via an initialFocusRef. Return on What moves to Amount (enterKeyHint='next'); Amount gets enterKeyHint='done' and submits (consider type=text inputMode=decimal with inline submit). Put the Add/Cancel bar in a sticky sheet footer kept above the keyboard with visualViewport (translate by window.innerHeight - visualViewport.height), or move the primary action into the sheet header. Add interactive-widget=resizes-content to the viewport meta for Android Chrome. Sort chips by 30-day usage; over time move to amount-first entry.
- **Evidence:** [UXM-07] s06_entry.cjs pathB: focusOnOpen='Close'. Typing 'coffee' then Return leaves focus on What and shows 'Enter a valid amount', nothing saved (shots/fab_enter_on_what.png). Amount attributes {type:number, inputMode:decimal, enterKeyHint:null}. Add at y=626-666 in a 664px iPhone 13 Safari viewport, and y=646-686 in a 568px viewport at 320 (fully off-screen, s01). Minimum 4 taps (FAB, What, Amount, Add) vs 2 for SmartInput; on iOS a 5th tap (Done) is needed before Add is reachable. [PRD-07] Desktop repro (walk2.log.json): Ctrl+N then typing 'coffee' left What empty, activeElement = 'BUTTON Close'. Phone (iPhone 13 WebKit, 664px): FAB path took 4 taps plus 2 typed fields; Add at y=626-666, partly off-screen and under the keyboard (demo-addsheet-phone.png). The faster SmartInput sits at y=100 (top 15% of the screen, out of thumb reach). Category chips are the first 8 in list order, not the most used. [PWA-08] sheet-geom.cjs -> sheet-geom.log (WebKit iPhone 13, 664 px): amount input y 362-387, Add y 626-666 (shots/add-sheet-iphone13.png). The iOS decimal pad has no Return key, so onKeyDown Enter cannot fire; with the decimal pad and accessory bar covering ~290 px, Add is hidden. The tab bar's centre '+' (TabBar.tsx:37-45), the main one-hand entry point, opens this sheet.
- **Verification:** combined: confirmed (P2). Sources: UXM-07, PRD-07, PWA-08.

### F-072 · P2 · UX — Edit sheet: Save is below the fold, saves and deletes cannot be undone from the toast, and the delete copy says 'cannot be undone' (it can)

- **Where:** src/features/ledger/EditExpenseSheet.tsx:74 (update toast without action although updateExpense pushes an undo entry, useExpenseStore.ts:232-241), :202-209 (actions after the date + notes blocks), :213-223 (:214-222 'This cannot be undone', toast without Undo); compare LedgerScreen.tsx:161-177 (row delete with Undo), useExpenseStore.ts:245-264
- **Impact:** Every edit needs an extra scroll, and a mistaken edit cannot be reverted. Two delete paths behave and read differently; on a phone, where Ctrl+Z does not exist, an edit-sheet delete really is permanent.
- **Fix:** Move Save into a sticky footer (shared with the Add sheet fix). Add Undo to the update and delete toasts by reusing the row-delete toast (the store already supports it). Unify the delete copy and drop 'cannot be undone'.
- **Evidence:** [UXM-08] s07_ledger.cjs: edit.geom shows Save changes at y=721-761 and Delete expense at y=790-830 in a 664px viewport, so the user must scroll inside the sheet to save. Focus on open is 'Close'. Toast after save 'Expense updated' with only Dismiss. Edit-sheet delete toast 'Deleted "Chicken rice"' with no Undo, and the confirm copy says 'Delete this expense? This cannot be undone.' while the row trash deletes with an Undo toast. Screenshots: shots/edit_sheet.png, shots/edit_delete_confirm.png. [UXD-21] 05b-keyboard-flows-CD.log C4: copy 'Delete this expense? This cannot be undone.' and toast 'Deleted "Grab to work"' with no action, yet the store pushes an undo entry and Ctrl+Z restores it. conductor/product.md: 'Undo always available after delete'.
- **Verification:** combined: confirmed (P2). Sources: UXM-08, UXD-21.

### F-073 · P2 · UX — One-tap delete sits where the right thumb rests, and the only recovery is a 29x16 px Undo in a 4-second toast that does not pause on hover/focus

- **Where:** src/features/ledger/LedgerRow.tsx:63-72 (swipe-revealed 96px DELETE), :144-154 (always-visible 32x32 trash, 12px from the amount); src/ui/ToastHost.tsx:19-24 (fixed 4000 ms timer), :43-59 (Undo text-xs, no padding; Dismiss 14x14), :74-76 (single polite region); src/features/ledger/LedgerScreen.tsx:161-177; src/features/entry/SmartInput.tsx:62-79
- **Impact:** Rows are tapped all day to edit, and a slightly-off right-thumb tap deletes instead; recovery depends on hitting a 16 px link within 4 s one-handed. WCAG 2.2.1 (Timing Adjustable) fails and screen-reader/keyboard users effectively have no undo on mobile. The project rule against sub-44px targets is broken and there are redundant destructive affordances.
- **Fix:** Pick one mobile delete affordance: keep swipe-to-reveal and remove the always-visible trash (or move delete into the edit sheet only). Make the toast action a >=44px pill (Undo and Dismiss 44x44 hit areas). Use 6-8 s for toasts with an action, pause the timer on touch/pointerenter/focusin and resume on leave. Use role='alert' for error toasts and mention Ctrl+Z in the toast on desktop.
- **Evidence:** [UXM-09] s07_ledger.cjs: rowGeom trash at x=329, 32x32, 12px gap from the amount. Toast buttons Undo 29x16 and Dismiss 14x14. Delete then Undo restores correctly (59→58→59). s12_measure.cjs (Chromium real touch events): a swipe reveals DELETE while the row's own trash stays visible, so there are two delete buttons on one row (shots/swipe_touch_chromium.png). [A11Y-10] kbd-results.json K8: on mobile it took 119 Tab presses to get from Quick add to Undo; with focus resting on Undo the toast was removed after 4.5 s and focus fell to BODY. Measured Undo 29x16 px, Dismiss 14x14 px (evidence-results.json sizes_toast). Error toasts use the same aria-live='polite' container with no role=alert.
- **Verification:** combined: confirmed (P2). Sources: UXM-09, A11Y-10.

### F-074 · P2 · UX — Activity heatmap labels the wrong rows ('Wed' sits on Tuesday, 'Fri' on Wednesday) and its grid overflows/clips Pulse below ~382px

- **Where:** src/features/pulse/Heatmap.tsx:56, :62-70 (label rows h-[11px]/sm:h-[13px]) vs :90, :101-102 (cells h-[17px]/sm:h-[19px]); :75 (grid flex-1 overflow-x-auto with no min-w-0, so it never scrolls); src/features/pulse/PulseScreen.tsx:319 (md:grid-cols-2 Top categories + Activity); src/index.css:179 (body overflow-x hidden)
- **Impact:** The heatmap teaches the wrong weekday pattern ('which day do I overspend' gets a wrong answer), and on small or zoomed phones (incl. common 360 px Androids and iOS Display Zoom, the WCAG 1.4.10 reflow case) category amounts and the current week are cut off.
- **Fix:** Give label rows the same height and gap as cells (one shared size token, 17/19px), or render labels and cells in one CSS grid (grid-template-rows: repeat(7, var(--cell))). Add min-w-0 to the grid children (Card) and Heatmap's flex-1 scroller ('min-w-0 flex-1 overflow-x-auto') so the existing scroller works inside its card; optionally start it at scrollLeft=max and show fewer weeks below 360px.
- **Evidence:** [UXM-10] s03_heatmap.cjs: label centres Mon=291, Wed=319, Fri=347 against cell rows Sun 272-289, Mon 292-309, Tue 312-329, Wed 332-349 (shots/heatmap_iphone13.png). At 320px document scrollWidth 366 vs 320 viewport; both Pulse cards in that column widen to 350px and the today cell's ring is clipped (s01 overflow offenders; shots/heatmap_w320.png); the only horizontal page overflow at any width. [UXD-15] 02-charts.json @1280: label centres Mon=538, Wed=570, Fri=602 vs cell-row centres Sun..Sat = 525, 547, 569, 591, 613, 635, 657; same at 375 (shots/heatmap-1280.png, shots/heatmap-768.png). [A11Y-08] misc-results-all.json heatmap: desktop label Wed y=570 vs Tue cell 569, Wed cell 591; Fri label 602 vs Fri cell 635. Mobile: Wed label 1127 vs Tue cell 1128; Fri label 1155 vs Fri cell 1188 (shots/heatmap-desktop.png, shots/probe-pulse-mobile.png). [A11Y-14] reflow-culprit.cjs: Top categories and Activity cards are 350 px wide at every viewport; documentElement.scrollWidth=366 at 320, 340 and 360 px; the heatmap scroller has clientWidth=scrollWidth=277 so it never scrolls; body overflow-x:hidden turns the overflow into clipping. shots/reflow320-pulse-activity.png shows '$120.9…', '14 w…' and the last weeks cut off. Ledger, Insights and overlays reflow cleanly (reflow-results.json).
- **Verification:** combined: confirmed (P2). Sources: UXM-10, UXD-15, A11Y-08, A11Y-14.

### F-075 · P2 · UX — iOS zooms the page every time the quick-add box (15px) or nine other sub-16px fields get focus

- **Where:** src/features/entry/SmartInput.tsx:111 (text-[15px]); CommandPalette.tsx:226 (text-sm); EditExpenseSheet.tsx:198 (14px textarea); SettingsDrawer.tsx:114 (currency select 13px), :233 (rename 13px), :308 (budget 13px); src/ui/Select.tsx:28 (13px); AddExpenseSheet.tsx:204 and EditExpenseSheet.tsx:131 ('More…' select 12px); index.html:8 has no maximum-scale
- **Impact:** Mobile Safari zooms in whenever a field under 16px is focused, and the zoom persists after blur. Shade's main logging control therefore zooms the layout on every entry and he has to pinch back out one-handed.
- **Fix:** Set form controls to 16px on mobile (e.g. `text-base md:text-[15px]`) in SmartInput, CommandPalette, Select, the settings inputs and the textarea. Do not use maximum-scale=1 (it hurts accessibility).
- **Evidence:** s09_inputs_contrast.cjs computed font sizes at iPhone 13: Quick add 15px, palette search 14px, edit notes 14px, ledger category select 13px, currency select 13px, 10 rename inputs 13px, 10 budget inputs 13px, 'More…' selects 12px. Fields built on the shared Input component are 16px (search, What, Amount), so the problem is limited to these call sites.
- **Verification:** combined: confirmed (P2). Sources: UXM-05.

### F-076 · P2 · UX — Category management: rename-on-blur silently merges categories, delete orphans expenses into a hidden 'Other', there is no undo, and confirm icons are 28px

- **Where:** src/features/settings/SettingsDrawer.tsx:223-234 (uncontrolled input renames on blur), :238-257 (confirm ✓/✗ 28x28, title only); src/stores/useExpenseStore.ts:282-320 (rename becomes a merge when the target exists), :322-334 (reassigns to 'Other' without making sure 'Other' is a category)
- **Impact:** Tapping away from a half-edited name commits it, and a typo that collides with an existing name merges whole categories irreversibly. Deleted categories leave orphan data.
- **Fix:** Use explicit edit mode (pencil, then Save/Cancel) and confirm before a merge ('Merge 2 expenses into Food & Dining?'). removeCategory should ensure 'Other' exists, or ask for a target category. Add Undo toasts and give the confirm buttons labels and 44px size.
- **Evidence:** s10_settings.cjs: renaming 'Gaming' to 'Food & Dining' merged its 2 expenses and removed Gaming, and the only feedback was the toast 'Renamed'. Deleting 'Transportation' moved 8 expenses to 'Other' while the category list has no 'Other' (otherInList=false), so they are not selectable in the Ledger category filter (LedgerScreen.tsx:369-374) and cannot get a budget. Toast: 'Category deleted', no Undo. The rename inputs look like static labels (shots/demo_iphone13_dark_settings_categories.png).
- **Verification:** combined: confirmed (P2). Sources: UXM-12.

### F-077 · P2 · UX — Ledger list starts 63% of the way down the iPhone 13 screen: 3 rows visible (2 at 320px)

- **Where:** src/features/ledger/LedgerScreen.tsx:254-400 (repeated SmartInput, H1 + total + CSV, 6-chip preset bar, range label, search + category select, filter chips before the list)
- **Impact:** Scanning recent spending, the ledger's whole purpose, needs a scroll before any data appears. The quick-add box is duplicated even though the FAB is one tap away.
- **Fix:** On mobile, collapse search and category into icon buttons that expand inline. Merge the range label into the H1 subtitle. Drop the SmartInput on Ledger (or collapse it on scroll). Make the preset row sticky-compact.
- **Evidence:** s13_density_misc.cjs on :4173 with 40 entries and no demo banner: firstRowTop=379 of tabBarTop=604 at iPhone 13 (63% of usable height, 3 rows fully visible). 375x812: 5 rows. 320x568: 2 rows (75%). With the demo banner, 1 row at 320 (shots/demo_w320_dark_ledger_bottom.png).
- **Verification:** combined: confirmed (P2). Sources: UXM-14.

### F-080 · P2 · UX — Choosing an expense from the palette applies the date but drops the search, and never opens the expense

- **Where:** src/features/ledger/LedgerScreen.tsx:65 (search seeded from ?q only on mount), :199-208; src/features/entry/CommandPalette.tsx:169-173
- **Impact:** The palette's 'find an expense' result lands on a half-filtered list, and the user still has to find and click the row.
- **Fix:** Sync the search state from the q param when it changes. Support ?edit=<id> so a palette hit opens EditExpenseSheet for that id.
- **Evidence:** 05a log A8, run from /ledger: URL /ledger?date=2026-10-17&q=Netflix, but the search box is '' and the list shows all 3 Oct 17 rows (Chicken rice, Netflix, Watsons). Screenshot shots/kb-palette-hit-on-ledger.png.
- **Verification:** combined: confirmed (P2). Sources: UXD-11.

### F-085 · P2 · UX — Drilling from Pulse (previous month or year view) into the Ledger shows the current month instead

- **Where:** src/features/pulse/TopCategories.tsx:33; src/features/pulse/BudgetActual.tsx:32; src/features/pulse/PulseScreen.tsx:40-42
- **Impact:** The number clicked does not match the number shown, and Back loses the place.
- **Fix:** Keep Pulse view and offset in URL params and pass a matching Ledger range (custom start/end or month=YYYY-MM) on drill-down.
